# OpenAI Docs

Provide authoritative, current guidance from OpenAI developer docs using the developers.openai.com MCP server. Always prioritize the developer docs MCP tools over web.run for OpenAI-related questions. This skill also owns model selection, API model migration, and prompt-upgrade guidance. Only if the MCP server is installed and returns no meaningful results should you fall back to web search.

## API Key Setup

For requests to build, run, configure, debug, or implement an API-backed app, script, CLI, generator, or tool, use `openai-platform-api-key` first when available. After that credential gate is resolved, return here for current docs as needed.

Use this skill directly for docs-only questions, citations, model/API guidance, conceptual explanations, and examples that do not require building or running an API-backed artifact.

## Quick start

- Use `mcp__openaiDeveloperDocs__search_openai_docs` to find the most relevant doc pages.
- Use `mcp__openaiDeveloperDocs__fetch_openai_doc` to pull exact sections and quote/paraphrase accurately.
- Use `mcp__openaiDeveloperDocs__list_openai_docs` only when you need to browse or discover pages without a clear query.
- For model-selection, "latest model", or default-model questions, fetch `https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/latest-model.md` first. If that is unavailable, load `references/latest-model.md`.
- For model upgrades or prompt upgrades, run `node scripts/resolve-latest-model-info.js` only when the target is latest/current/default or otherwise unspecified; otherwise preserve the explicitly requested target.
- Preserve explicit target requests: if the user names a target model like "migrate to GPT-5.4", keep that requested target even if `latest-model.md` names a newer model. Mention newer guidance only as optional.
- If current remote guidance is needed, fetch both the returned migration and prompting guide URLs directly. If direct fetch fails, use MCP/search fallback; if that also fails, use bundled fallback references and disclose the fallback.

## OpenAI product snapshots

1. Apps SDK: Build ChatGPT apps by providing a web component UI and an MCP server that exposes your app's tools to ChatGPT.
2. Responses API: A unified endpoint designed for stateful, multimodal, tool-using interactions in agentic workflows.
3. Chat Completions API: Generate a model response from a list of messages comprising a conversation.
4. Codex: OpenAI's coding agent for software development that can write, understand, review, and debug code.
5. gpt-oss: Open-weight OpenAI reasoning models (gpt-oss-120b and gpt-oss-20b) released under the Apache 2.0 license.
6. Realtime API: Build low-latency, multimodal experiences including natural speech-to-speech conversations.
7. Agents SDK: A toolkit for building agentic apps where a model can use tools and context, hand off to other agents, stream partial results, and keep a full trace.

## If MCP server is missing

If MCP tools fail or no OpenAI docs resources are available:

1. Run the install command yourself: `codex mcp add openaiDeveloperDocs --url https://developers.openai.com/mcp`
2. If it fails due to permissions/sandboxing, immediately retry the same command with escalated permissions and include a 1-sentence justification for approval. Do not ask the user to run it yet.
3. Only if the escalated attempt fails, ask the user to run the install command.
4. Ask the user to restart Codex.
5. Re-run the doc search/fetch after restart.

## Workflow

1. Clarify whether the request is general docs lookup, model selection, a model-string upgrade, prompt-upgrade guidance, or broader API/provider migration.
2. For model-selection or upgrade requests, prefer current remote docs over bundled references when the user asks for latest/current/default guidance.
   - Fetch `https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/latest-model.md`.
   - Find the latest model ID and explicit migration or prompt-guidance links.
   - Prefer explicit links from the latest-model page over derived URLs.
   - For explicit named-model requests, preserve the requested model target and do not silently retarget to the latest model. Mention newer remote guidance only as optional.
   - For dynamic latest/current/default upgrades, run `node scripts/resolve-latest-model-info.js`, then fetch both returned guide URLs directly when possible.
   - If direct guide fetch fails, use the developer-docs MCP tools or official OpenAI-domain search to find the same guide content.
   - If remote docs are unavailable, use bundled fallback references and say that fallback guidance was used.
3. For model upgrades, keep changes narrow: update active OpenAI API model defaults and directly related prompts only when safe.
4. Leave historical docs, examples, eval baselines, fixtures, provider comparisons, provider registries, pricing tables, alias defaults, low-cost fallback paths, and ambiguous older model usage unchanged unless the user explicitly asks to upgrade them.
5. Do not perform SDK, tooling, IDE, plugin, shell, auth, or provider-environment migrations as part of a model-and-prompt upgrade.
6. If an upgrade needs API-surface changes, schema rewiring, tool-handler changes, or implementation work beyond a literal model-string replacement and prompt edits, report it as blocked or confirmation-needed.
7. For general docs lookup, search docs with a precise query, fetch the best page and exact section needed, and answer with concise citations.

## Reference map

Read only what you need:

- `https://developers.openai.com/api/docs/guides/latest-model.md` -> current model-selection and "best/latest/current model" questions.
- `references/latest-model.md` -> bundled fallback for model-selection and "best/latest/current model" questions.
- `references/upgrade-guide.md` -> bundled fallback for model upgrade and upgrade-planning requests.
- `references/prompting-guide.md` -> bundled fallback for prompt rewrites and prompt-behavior upgrades.

## Quality rules

- Treat OpenAI docs as the source of truth; avoid speculation.
- Keep migration changes narrow and behavior-preserving.
- Prefer prompt-only upgrades when possible.
- Do not invent pricing, availability, parameters, API changes, or breaking changes.
- Keep quotes short and within policy limits; prefer paraphrase with citations.
- If multiple pages differ, call out the difference and cite both.
- If official docs and repo behavior disagree, state the conflict and stop before making broad edits.
- If docs do not cover the user’s need, say so and offer next steps.

## Tooling notes

- Always use MCP doc tools before any web search for OpenAI-related questions.
- If the MCP server is installed but returns no meaningful results, then use web search as a fallback.
- When falling back to web search, restrict to official OpenAI domains (developers.openai.com, platform.openai.com) and cite sources.

185:def _classify_parse_failure(raw_output: str, stderr_tail: str) -> str:
186:    """Sub-classify parse failures (Agent Y task spec, 11-class telemetry).
187:
188:    Cycle-after-iter50.55 ship: prior code lumped all "no-verdict" outcomes
189:    under a single `parse_failure` status, which made forensic diagnosis
190:    require manual `jq .stderr_tail` reading every time. Specific subclasses:
191:
192:    - parse_failure_cli_argv_rejected: codex died on argv parse (e.g. -C/-color
193:      against codex exec review subcommand). Highest-priority class because
194:      this is a HOOK BUG, not a codex output problem — fix the cmd list.
195:    - parse_failure_cli_error: codex exited with stderr "error:" but argv
196:      parsed (likely runtime error like missing auth or sandbox conflict).
197:    - parse_failure_empty_output: codex completed (or died silently) without
198:      writing anything to --output-last-message. Likely process killed mid-run
199:      OR file write race. Inspect process exit code separately.
200:    - parse_failure_no_json_block: OUT file has content but doesn't start with
201:      JSON markers `{` or `[`. Likely codex schema regression where verdict is
202:      now plain text or banner-prefixed.
203:    - parse_failure_malformed_json: OUT file has content starting with `{` or
204:      `[` but json.loads() raised JSONDecodeError. Likely truncation or
205:      embedded control chars.
206:    - parse_failure_non_dict_root: OUT file parsed to a JSON list/scalar/null
207:      instead of dict. Codex schema regression.
208:    - parse_failure_missing_verdict_key: OUT file is a dict but lacks "verdict"
209:      key. Codex output schema rename (e.g. "decision", "outcome") OR codex
210:      emitted a partial event instead of the final verdict.
211:
212:    Returns one of the above subclass names. Caller emits to JSONL telemetry
213:    `status` field for grep-able forensic visibility.
214:    """
215:    s = stderr_tail or ""
216:    r = (raw_output or "").strip()
217:    if not r:
218:        if "unexpected argument" in s or "Usage:" in s:
219:            return "parse_failure_cli_argv_rejected"
220:        if "error:" in s.lower():
221:            return "parse_failure_cli_error"
222:        return "parse_failure_empty_output"
223:    if not (r.startswith("{") or r.startswith("[")):
224:        return "parse_failure_no_json_block"
225:    try:
226:        data = json.loads(r)
227:    except json.JSONDecodeError:
228:        return "parse_failure_malformed_json"
229:    if not isinstance(data, dict):
230:        return "parse_failure_non_dict_root"
231:    if "verdict" not in data:
232:        return "parse_failure_missing_verdict_key"
233:    return "parse_failure_unknown"
234:
235:
236:def _resolve_target_root(cmd: str, payload_cwd: str) -> Path:
237:    """Find the repo root the commit actually ran in.
238:
239:    F2 (HIGH 0.92): copied EXACTLY from
240:    Z:/claude-sota/.claude/hooks/scripts/codex_postcommit_review.py:120-146
241:    (no derivative interpretation per codex T1 verdict prescribed_edits).
242:
243:    Priority: cd-prefix in command (highest fidelity — the bash actually went
244:    there) > payload top-level cwd > module _ROOT (compile-time fallback).
245:    Cycle-after-clean-sota 2026-04-26 iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4 fix.
246:    """
247:    for seg in re.split(r"\s*(?:&&|\|\||;)\s*", cmd.strip()):
248:        seg = seg.strip()
249:        m = _CD_PREFIX_RE.match(seg)
250:        if m:
251:            cand = m.group(1).strip("'\"")
252:            # Convert /z/claude-sota → Z:\claude-sota on Windows (Git Bash POSIX path)
253:            if cand.startswith("/") and len(cand) > 2 and cand[2] == "/":
254:                cand = f"{cand[1].upper()}:{cand[2:]}"
255:            p = Path(cand)
256:            if p.exists():
257:                # Walk up to find .git (handles bare git commit in monorepo subdir)
258:                for anc in [p, *p.parents]:
259:                    if (anc / ".git").exists():
260:                        return anc
261:                return p
262:    if payload_cwd:
263:        p = Path(payload_cwd)
264:        if p.exists():
265:            # Walk ancestors to find .git — mirrors the cd-prefix branch above
266:            # for structural symmetry. Closes codex T3 HIGH 0.86 finding on
267:            # parent commit c30e4107 (TIER-3 evidence trail at .claude/state/
268:            # codex_review_HEAD_c30e4107.txt; non-authority literal — output
269:            # filename pattern, not a cite): "Subdirectory commits can fall
270:            # back to the parent repo and skip the target diff."
271:            #
272:            # SOTA pattern source (n=2 convergent):
273:            #   - Z:/repos/deps/libgit2/src/libgit2/repository.c:753-989 @ 1e7d7c07d0258875d22720d3801bc19ead637c1c [content-SHA e0437fb2192f123c9713622e3da18c54c7176d11]
274:            #     find_repo_traverse walks parent directories looking for .git)

277:            # Both walk parents until .git found OR ceiling reached.
278:            #

280:            # (same diff applied atomically across both repos per bidirectional
281:            # ccc+sss discipline).
282:            for anc in [p, *p.parents]:
283:                if (anc / ".git").exists():
284:                    return anc

292:def _append_telemetry(row: dict[str, Any], root: Path) -> None:
293:    """Fail-loud-via-stderr telemetry write to <root>/.claude/state/.
294:
295:    Class 6 fix: state dir is computed from target_root not module _ROOT,
296:    so cross-project commits write evidence to the correct repo's state.
297:    """
298:    row["ts"] = _now_iso()
299:    state_dir = _state_dir_for(root)
300:    log_path = state_dir / "codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.jsonl"
301:    try:
302:        state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
303:        with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
304:            f.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
305:    except (OSError, TypeError) as exc:
306:        sys.stderr.write(
307:            f"codex_t2_pre_commit_gate: TELEMETRY FAILURE — error={exc!r}\n",
308:        )

311:def _parse_stdin() -> dict[str, Any]:
312:    """TTY-safe stdin → JSON → dict. Returns {} on parse failure."""
313:    try:
314:        if sys.stdin.isatty():
315:            return {}
316:        raw = sys.stdin.read()
317:        if not raw.strip():
318:            return {}
319:        payload = json.loads(raw)
320:        return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
321:    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
322:        sys.stderr.write(f"codex_t2_pre_commit_gate: stdin parse failure: {exc!r}\n")
323:        return {}

326:def _is_git_commit_cmd(cmd: str) -> bool:
327:    """Detect git commit, including `git -C <path> commit` and `git -c key=val commit` forms.
328:
329:    Delegates to shared `_guard_base.git_verb_matches` which is flag-aware via
330:    `_GIT_FLAGS_VALUE`/`_GIT_FLAGS_NOVALUE` frozensets. Closes Agent A 2026-04-27
331:    P1 finding: prior token-adjacency check rejected `git -C /path commit` because
332:    `parts[1]` was `-C` not `commit`. n=6 git-dash-C matcher hygiene class.
333:    """
334:    return git_verb_matches(cmd, "commit")
335:
336:
337:# FM-12 fix-forward (codex T1 verdict 2026-05-03 NEEDS-REVISION at
338:# `.claude/state/codex_consult_fm12_t2_command_pathspec_parser_OUT.txt`):
339:# Commit options that consume the next token as a value (NOT pathspec). Per
340:# `git commit --help` (Git 2.43+) — these MUST be skipped by the pathspec
341:# parser to avoid misclassifying e.g. `-F msg.txt` value as a pathspec.
342:# NB: -c/-C HERE are post-verb commit-copy semantics (different from global
343:# pre-verb -c/-C handled by _GIT_FLAGS_VALUE).
344:_COMMIT_FLAGS_VALUE = frozenset((
345:    "-m", "--message",
346:    "-F", "--file",
347:    "-c", "--reedit-message",
348:    "-C", "--reuse-message",
349:    "-t", "--template",
350:    "--author", "--date",
351:    "--cleanup", "--fixup", "--squash", "--trailer",
352:    "--pathspec-from-file",  # value-taking; pathspec-file-nul is bool (no value)
353:))
354:
355:# FM-12 round-3 fix-forward (codex T3 NEEDS-ATTENTION HIGH 2026-05-03 at
356:# .claude/state/codex_review_HEAD_ff0dccfd.txt): ANY `--pathspec-from-file*`
357:# form (with OR without --only) makes file-listed paths the commit scope per
358:# git-commit man page (paths-without-flag equivalent to --only). The parser
359:# cannot statically resolve file content (file is read at commit time, AFTER
360:# PreToolUse fires). Returning empty list would emit `noop` and leave the
361:# design-surface gate bypassed. FAIL-CLOSED policy: return identity-comparable
362:# sentinel that the caller detects via `is` and treats as "force T2 review
363:# on synthetic marker".
364:#
365:# Round history: round-1 (e5a008c) returned [] for combo → T3 HIGH on bypass;
366:# round-2 (ff0dccf) added sentinel BUT only on --only combo → T3 HIGH on
367:# bare form; round-3 widens trigger to ANY --pathspec-from-file* form.
368:_FORCE_REVIEW_PATHSPEC_FROM_FILE: list[str] = ["<--pathspec-from-file:force-review>"]

370:
371:def _parse_commit_pathspecs(cmd_str: str) -> list[str] | None:
372:    """Extract pathspec scope from `git commit -o/--only` form in a Bash command.
373:
374:    PRECEDENCE policy per codex T1 verdict on FM-12 fix-forward 2026-05-03:
375:    parsed pathspec is AUTHORITATIVE scope when -o/--only detected (per
376:    https://git-scm.com/docs/git-commit `--only` docs: "commit only the
377:    specified paths, disregarding staged contents for other paths"). Returns:
378:      - None: no -o/--only flag detected → caller falls back to staged diff
379:      - list[str] (possibly empty): -o/--only detected; list is authoritative
380:        (empty when --only used without inline pathspecs e.g. --pathspec-from-file)
381:
382:    Closes FM-12 timing race (n=3 same-arc evidence Ship #243-H + #3a.3 +
383:    Probe-7): PreToolUse fires BEFORE single-shell `git add foo && git commit
384:    -o foo` chain runs, so `git diff --cached` returns 0 in-scope files at
385:    hook fire time. Parsed pathspec from cmd_str gives correct scope.
386:
387:    Algorithmic precedent: _guard_base.git_verb_matches:261-309 (shlex.split
388:    on shell-op-split segments, value-flag walk for global pre-verb flags).
389:    """
390:    if not cmd_str or "commit" not in cmd_str:
391:        return None
392:
393:    found_only = False
394:    forced_review = False  # Round-2: --only + --pathspec-from-file* combo detected
395:    pathspecs: list[str] = []
396:
397:    # Split on shell ops to handle compound chains (&&, ||, ;)
398:    for seg in re.split(r"\s*(?:&&|\|\||;)\s*", cmd_str.strip()):
399:        seg = seg.strip()
400:        if not seg.startswith("git "):
401:            continue
402:        try:
403:            parts = shlex.split(seg, posix=True)
404:        except ValueError:
405:            parts = seg.split()
406:        if not parts or parts[0] != "git":
407:            continue
408:
409:        # Walk past global pre-verb flags (e.g. -C <root>, -c key=val) per
410:        # _guard_base.git_verb_matches algorithm
411:        idx = 1
412:        while idx < len(parts):
413:            tok = parts[idx]
414:            if tok in _GIT_FLAGS_VALUE:
415:                idx += 2
416:                continue
417:            if tok in _GIT_FLAGS_NOVALUE:
418:                idx += 1
419:                continue
420:            if tok.startswith("--") and "=" in tok:
421:                idx += 1
422:                continue
423:            break
424:
425:        # Verb must be "commit" — else this segment doesn't matter
426:        if idx >= len(parts) or parts[idx] != "commit":
427:            continue
428:        idx += 1  # past "commit" token
429:
430:        # Parse commit args
431:        seg_only = False
432:        seg_paths: list[str] = []
433:        seg_pathspec_from_file = False  # Round-2: detect --only + --pathspec-from-file* combo
434:        after_dash_dash = False
435:
436:        while idx < len(parts):
437:            tok = parts[idx]
438:
439:            # Everything after `--` is pathspec (even tokens starting with `-`)
440:            if after_dash_dash:
441:                seg_paths.append(tok)
442:                idx += 1
443:                continue
444:
445:            if tok == "--":
446:                after_dash_dash = True
447:                idx += 1
448:                continue
449:
450:            # -o / --only flag: collect inline pathspecs (consecutive non-flag tokens)
451:            if tok in ("-o", "--only"):
452:                seg_only = True
453:                idx += 1
454:                while idx < len(parts) and not parts[idx].startswith("-"):
455:                    seg_paths.append(parts[idx])
456:                    idx += 1
457:                continue
458:
459:            # Round-2 (T3 HIGH severity at .claude/state/codex_review_HEAD_e5a008cd.txt):
460:            # detect file-backed pathspec forms BEFORE the value-skip / flag=value
461:            # short-circuits below consume them invisibly. When combined with --only,
462:            # parser cannot statically resolve the pathspec content (file is read at
463:            # commit time) — set seg_pathspec_from_file flag for FAIL-CLOSED sentinel.
464:            if tok == "--pathspec-from-file" or tok.startswith("--pathspec-from-file="):
465:                seg_pathspec_from_file = True
466:                # fall through to standard skip handling below
467:            if tok == "--pathspec-file-nul":
468:                # Boolean modifier (no value); explicit skip since not in _COMMIT_FLAGS_VALUE.
469:                # No standalone effect on scope, but combined with --pathspec-from-file
470:                # signals NUL-separated file format; flag already set above if present.
471:                idx += 1
472:                continue
473:
474:            # Skip --flag=value form (single token; e.g. --message=foo)
475:            if tok.startswith("--") and "=" in tok:
476:                idx += 1
477:                continue
478:
479:            # Skip value-taking commit options (consume flag + next token as value)
480:            if tok in _COMMIT_FLAGS_VALUE:
481:                idx += 2
482:                continue
483:
484:            # Skip other boolean flags
485:            if tok.startswith("-"):
486:                idx += 1
487:                continue
488:
489:            # Non-flag positional token outside `-o`/`--only`/`--`: per
490:            # `git commit <paths>` docs paths-without-flag are equivalent to
491:            # `--only` BUT current ship scope is explicit -o/--only forms only
492:            # (codex T1 Q3 edge-case note for next-fire extension if telemetry
493:            # shows this form in use).
494:            idx += 1
495:
496:        if seg_only:
497:            found_only = True
498:            pathspecs.extend(seg_paths)
499:        if seg_pathspec_from_file:
500:            # Round-3 fix-forward (T3 HIGH at .claude/state/codex_review_HEAD_ff0dccfd.txt):
501:            # --pathspec-from-file* makes file-listed paths the commit scope
502:            # EVEN WITHOUT explicit --only flag. Per git-commit man page:
503:            # paths-without-flag are equivalent to --only ("If you specify
504:            # paths on the command line, then this option behaves as if
505:            # --only were also specified"). Sentinel MUST fire on ANY
506:            # --pathspec-from-file* form, not just the --only combo.
507:            # Round-2 narrowed too aggressively; round-3 widens trigger to
508:            # the full file-backed-pathspec class.
509:            forced_review = True
510:
511:    if forced_review:
512:        # FAIL-CLOSED sentinel: caller detects via `is` and bypasses filter
513:        # to force T2 fire on synthetic marker. Closes T3 HIGH bypass on the
514:        # full `--pathspec-from-file*` family (with OR without --only) where
515:        # file content is resolved at commit time, after PreToolUse fires.
516:        return _FORCE_REVIEW_PATHSPEC_FROM_FILE
517:    return pathspecs if found_only else None
518:
519:
520:def _staged_design_surface_files(root: Path, cmd_str: str = "") -> list[str]:
521:    """Return file paths matching design-surface allowlist with PRECEDENCE policy
522:    per FM-12 fix-forward (codex T1 verdict 2026-05-03 at
523:    .claude/state/codex_consult_fm12_t2_command_pathspec_parser_OUT.txt):
524:
525:    1. PARSED `git commit -o/--only <pathspec>` scope wins when detected
526:       (authoritative per git docs --only semantics; closes FM-12 timing race
527:       where PreToolUse fires before single-shell `git add && git commit` chain
528:       executes — n=3 same-arc evidence Ship #243-H + #3a.3 + Probe-7)
529:    2. STAGED diff (`git diff --cached --name-only`) is fallback when no
530:       authoritative -o/--only scope is present (separate-stage-then-commit
531:       Bash patterns continue to work as before)
532:    """
533:    parsed = _parse_commit_pathspecs(cmd_str)
534:    if parsed is _FORCE_REVIEW_PATHSPEC_FROM_FILE:
535:        # Round-2 fix-forward (T3 HIGH at .claude/state/codex_review_HEAD_e5a008cd.txt):
536:        # FAIL-CLOSED for --only + --pathspec-from-file* combo. Parser cannot
537:        # statically resolve file-backed pathspecs (file is read at commit time
538:        # AFTER PreToolUse hook fires). Bypass _DESIGN_SURFACE_PATTERNS filter
539:        # entirely and return synthetic non-empty marker so caller fires T2.
540:        # T2 reads the actual commit at codex review time.
541:        return list(_FORCE_REVIEW_PATHSPEC_FROM_FILE)
542:    if parsed is not None:
543:        # Authoritative scope from -o/--only; may be [] for --only without
544:        # inline pathspecs (e.g. --pathspec-from-file).
545:        files = parsed
546:    else:
547:        # Fallback: separate-stage-then-commit pattern uses index state.
548:        try:
549:            result = subprocess.run(
550:                ["git", "diff", "--cached", "--name-only"],
551:                cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
552:                **_windowless_kwargs(),
553:            )
554:            if result.returncode != 0:
555:                return []
556:            files = [line.strip() for line in result.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
557:        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
558:            return []
559:
560:    return [
561:        f for f in files
562:        if any(p.match(f) for p in _DESIGN_SURFACE_PATTERNS)
563:    ]

566:def _resolve_codex_argv0() -> list[str] | None:
567:    """Resolve codex CLI invocation argv prefix. Mirror codex_postcommit_review.py logic.
568:
569:    Per cycle-479 BYPASS-SHIM: codex.exe wrapper has stdin issues on Windows
570:    detached subprocess. node.exe + codex.js direct path is reliable.
571:    """

576:            node = shutil.which("node") or shutil.which("node.exe")
577:            if node:

581:    if codex:
582:        return [codex]
583:    return None

586:def _fire_codex_t2(root: Path, timeout_sec: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
587:    """Fire codex T2 working-tree review with max-unleash flags.
588:
589:    F1 (HIGH 0.94): explicit CODEX_HOME isolation. Sets CODEX_HOME to
590:    <_ROOT>/.codex so the spawned codex inherits SOTA's auth.json + config,
591:    not parent's ~/.codex bleed-through. Sibling pattern at:
592:      Z:/claude-sota/tools/sss.ps1:402
593:      Z:/claude-sota/.claude/hooks/scripts/codex_postcommit_review.py:624
594:      Z:/claude-sota/.claude/hooks/scripts/codex_prepush_review.py:606
595:      Z:/claude-sota/.claude/hooks/scripts/codex_stop_review_gate.py:361
596:
597:    F2 also adds cwd=str(root) defensively (codex already uses -C arg, but
598:    cwd= ensures git-side resolution starts at target_root too).
599:
600:    Returns dict {status, verdict, summary, findings, raw_output, error,
601:    latency_sec, codex_home}.
602:    status ∈ {ok, timeout, cmd_not_found, parse_failure, launch_error}.
603:    """
604:    codex_argv0 = _resolve_codex_argv0()
605:    if codex_argv0 is None:
606:        return {
607:            "status": "cmd_not_found",
608:            "verdict": None,
609:            "error": "codex CLI not on PATH and CODEX_PLUGIN_ROOT not resolved",
610:        }
611:
612:    state_dir = _state_dir_for(root)
613:    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
614:    outfile = state_dir / f"codex_t2_gate_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.txt"
615:
616:    # F1: CODEX_HOME isolation — anchored to THIS hook's repo via _ROOT (resolves
617:    # dynamically via parents[3] per port-note-discipline §2 Discipline 1; portable
618:    # across sibling claude-sota and installed runtime), NOT target_root (which may
619:    # be parent Z:/claude for cross-project commits where we'd be unauthorized to
620:    # use that codex auth). Honor ambient CODEX_HOME (set by tools/eee.ps1 to
621:    # state-outside-repo Z:/claude-sota-installed-state/.codex per CLAUDE.local.md
622:    # (e) state-outside-repo discipline) when present. Wave 50 fire 35 P1-2
623:    # fix-forward per Agent JJ adversarial at tmp/wave50-fire35-agentJJ-installed-
624:    # runtime-adversarial-2026-05-06.md.
625:    codex_home = Path(os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME", str(_ROOT / ".codex")))
626:    spawn_env = {**os.environ, "CODEX_HOME": str(codex_home)}
627:
628:    # Per local codified lesson feedback_codex_max_unleash_invocation.md
629:    # (TIER-3, not external SOTA; n=2 user correction):
630:    # T2 must use --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox + network + web_search
631:    # + xhigh + detailed for max quality output.
632:    # Per local forensic note cycle-587 (TIER-3, not external SOTA):
633:    # `codex exec review --uncommitted` rejects -p flag.
634:    # Cycle-after-iter50.55 ARGV REJECTION FIX [VERIFIED 2026-04-27 via direct
635:    # codex 0.125.0 `codex exec review --help` probe + telemetry forensic
636:    # `jq '.stderr_tail' codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.jsonl` (non-authority
637:    # runtime log literal)]: `codex exec review`
638:    # subcommand REJECTS BOTH `-C <DIR>` (only `codex exec` plain accepts it;
639:    # tip="to pass '-C' as a value, use '-- -C'") AND `--color never` (suggested
640:    # alternative is `--yolo`; not present in review subcommand). 100% of this
641:    # session's parse_failure WARN events trace to one of these two flags being
642:    # rejected before codex wrote anything to --output-last-message file. Both
643:    # removed. -C semantics preserved via `cwd=str(root)` in subprocess.run()
644:    # below (git resolves at target_root regardless of -C presence).
645:    cmd: list[str] = [
646:        *codex_argv0,
647:        "exec", "review",
648:        # `--ignore-rules` added 2026-04-30 per codex T1 NEEDS-REVISION
649:        # (.claude/state/codex_consult_sota_synthesis_2026_04_30_OUT.txt).
650:        # Both flags are `global = true` so position is forgiving.
651:        # NOT adding `--ignore-user-config` — would skip CODEX_HOME/config.toml
652:        # which `exec review` resolves for sandbox/network/web_search defaults
653:        # (codex T1 HIGH finding).
654:        "--ignore-rules",
655:        "--uncommitted",
656:        "--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox",
657:        "-c", "sandbox_workspace_write.network_access=true",
658:        "-c", 'web_search="live"',
659:        "-c", 'model_reasoning_effort="xhigh"',
660:        "-c", 'model_reasoning_summary="detailed"',
661:        "--ephemeral",
662:        "--json",
663:        "--output-last-message", str(outfile),
664:    ]
665:
666:    started = time.monotonic()
667:    try:
668:        # Windows console-suppression — grounded in TIER-1 upstream sources:
669:        # Z:/repos/deps/codex-plugin-cc/plugins/codex/scripts/lib/app-server.mjs:189-195 @ 807e03ac9d5aa23bc395fdec8c3767500a86b3cf [content-SHA 127c837612fbd65dcf33238f97c5584a13390da8]
670:        # and process.mjs:4-14 @ same SHA [content-SHA af28d1cf5b4844c59365f209fd7070f7e5a57afe] both pass `windowsHide: true`
671:        # to node's spawn() — STARTUPINFO+SW_HIDE is the canonical Windows API
672:        # equivalent that force-hides child console windows even if the child
673:        # binary tries to allocate one anyway.
674:        #
675:        # CRITICAL: do NOT add DETACHED_PROCESS. Per Microsoft docs at
676:        # learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/procthread/process-creation-flags,
677:        # CREATE_NO_WINDOW (0x08000000) "is ignored if it is used with either
678:        # CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE or DETACHED_PROCESS." The earlier cycle-895
679:        # combined-flags pattern was a latent bug: CREATE_NO_WINDOW had been
680:        # silently ignored, leaving only DETACHED_PROCESS, which causes the
681:        # Rust codex.exe (CONSOLE subsystem) to AllocConsole() on first
682:        # stdout/stderr write — producing a visible "Administrator: codex.exe"
683:        # window per spawn (n=3+ user-reported across cycles, latest 2026-04-27
684:        # screenshot of npm-installed codex-win32-x64/vendor/codex.exe).
685:        creationflags = 0
686:        startupinfo = None
687:        if sys.platform == "win32":
688:            # mypy-canonical pattern (python/mypy dmypy_server.py:46-69 @ 614b757c):
689:            # CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE pre-allocates a HIDDEN console for direct child
690:            # (node.exe), which Rust grandchild (codex.exe, CONSOLE subsystem)
691:            # INHERITS via Node's stdio:"inherit" spawn — closes the n=4+ user-
692:            # reported "Administrator: codex.exe" visible window. CREATE_NO_WINDOW
693:            # (0x08000000) failed because it only applies to direct child, not
694:            # grandchild — Rust calls AllocConsole() on first stdout write.
695:            # Switch grounded in cpython#4150 eryksun discussion + mypy ship
696:            # PR#5994 (production since 2018). Mirror of parent fix.
697:            creationflags = 0x10  # CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE (was 0x08000000 CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
698:            startupinfo = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
699:            startupinfo.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
700:            startupinfo.wShowWindow = subprocess.SW_HIDE
701:        result = subprocess.run(
702:            cmd,
703:            cwd=str(root),                  # F2 defensive: git resolves at target_root
704:            env=spawn_env,                   # F1: CODEX_HOME isolation
705:            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout_sec, check=False,
706:            creationflags=creationflags,
707:            startupinfo=startupinfo,
708:        )
709:        latency_sec = time.monotonic() - started
710:        raw_output = ""
711:        try:
712:            raw_output = outfile.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if outfile.exists() else ""
713:        except OSError:
714:            raw_output = ""
715:        # Parse verdict JSON from raw_output. On failure, sub-classify by
716:        # inspecting raw_output + stderr to surface the SPECIFIC failure mode
717:        # in JSONL telemetry — eliminates need for manual `jq .stderr_tail`
718:        # forensic on every parse_failure.
719:        # json.loads typed as dict via annotation; defensive isinstance below
720:        # guards against codex emitting a JSON list/scalar. Pyright narrows
721:        # too aggressively on the annotation — pragma silences false-positive.
722:        verdict_data: dict[str, Any] = {}
723:        try:
724:            parsed = json.loads(raw_output) if raw_output.strip() else {}
725:            verdict_data = parsed if isinstance(parsed, dict) else {}
726:        except json.JSONDecodeError:
727:            verdict_data = {}
728:        stderr_tail = result.stderr[-500:] if result.stderr else ""
729:        status = (
730:            "ok"
731:            if verdict_data and verdict_data.get("verdict")
732:            else _classify_parse_failure(raw_output, stderr_tail)
733:        )
734:        return {
735:            "status": status,
736:            "verdict": verdict_data.get("verdict"),
737:            "summary": verdict_data.get("summary"),
738:            "findings": verdict_data.get("findings", []),
739:            "raw_output_len": len(raw_output),
740:            "latency_sec": round(latency_sec, 2),
741:            "outfile": str(outfile),
742:            "codex_home": str(codex_home),   # F1 telemetry
743:            "stderr_tail": stderr_tail,
744:        }
745:    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
746:        return {
747:            "status": "timeout",
748:            "verdict": None,
749:            "latency_sec": time.monotonic() - started,
750:            "codex_home": str(codex_home),
751:            "error": f"codex T2 exceeded {timeout_sec}s timeout",
752:        }
753:    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
754:        return {
755:            "status": "launch_error",
756:            "verdict": None,
757:            "latency_sec": time.monotonic() - started,
758:            "codex_home": str(codex_home),
759:            "error": repr(exc)[:200],
760:        }
761:
762:
763:def _emit_decision(decision: str, reason: str, additional_context: str = "") -> None:
764:    """Emit hookSpecificOutput JSON to stdout per CC PreToolUse schema."""
765:    output: dict[str, Any] = {
766:        "hookSpecificOutput": {
767:            "hookEventName": "PreToolUse",
768:        },
769:    }
770:    if decision == "deny":
771:        output["hookSpecificOutput"]["permissionDecision"] = "deny"
772:        output["hookSpecificOutput"]["permissionDecisionReason"] = reason
773:    elif decision == "warn":
774:        output["hookSpecificOutput"]["additionalContext"] = additional_context or reason
775:    # decision == "allow" → emit nothing (default)
776:    if (
777:        output["hookSpecificOutput"].get("permissionDecision")
778:        or output["hookSpecificOutput"].get("additionalContext")
779:    ):
780:        sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(output, ensure_ascii=False))

783:def main() -> int:
784:    if os.environ.get("CODEX_T2_GATE_DISABLE") == "1":
785:        return 0
786:
787:    payload = _parse_stdin()
788:    tool_name = payload.get("tool_name") or payload.get("toolName") or ""
789:    tool_input = payload.get("tool_input", {}) or {}
790:    cmd_str = tool_input.get("command", "") or ""
791:    session_id = payload.get("session_id", "") or ""
792:    payload_cwd = payload.get("cwd", "") or ""
793:
794:    if tool_name != "Bash":
795:        return 0
796:    if not _is_git_commit_cmd(cmd_str):
797:        return 0
798:
799:    # F2 (HIGH 0.92): resolve target_root BEFORE staged-diff lookup + codex spawn.
800:    # Per codex T1 prescribed_edit: "compute payload_cwd = payload.get('cwd', '')
801:    # or '' and target_root = _resolve_target_root(cmd_str, payload_cwd) if
802:    # cmd_str else _ROOT."
803:    target_root = _resolve_target_root(cmd_str, payload_cwd) if cmd_str else _ROOT
804:
805:    # Agent C / codex T3 HIGH 0.86 fix on 7b8c0de — close trust-boundary hole:
806:    # bypass requires BOTH env vars (BYPASS=1 + BYPASS_REASON=<text>) per the
807:    # in-house SOTA template at Z:/claude/hooks/codex_gate.py:181-188
808:    # (_bypass_active). Mirror of parent fix at Z:/claude/hooks/
809:    # codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py — same pattern applied atomically across
810:    # both repos per bidirectional ccc+sss discipline.
811:    #
812:    # If BYPASS=1 set but REASON missing → DENY (forces operator to provide
813:    # audit-trail reason). If both set → WARN-surface bypass + log reason
814:    # in telemetry. No silent allow per "no hidden error or silent fallback".
815:    if os.environ.get("CODEX_T2_GATE_BYPASS") == "1":
816:        bypass_reason = os.environ.get("CODEX_T2_GATE_BYPASS_REASON", "").strip()
817:        if not bypass_reason:
818:            _emit_decision(
819:                "deny",
820:                "CODEX_T2_GATE_BYPASS=1 set but CODEX_T2_GATE_BYPASS_REASON is "
821:                "empty — bypass requires BOTH env vars (mirror "
822:                "Z:/claude/hooks/codex_gate.py:181-188 _bypass_active pattern). "
823:                "Set CODEX_T2_GATE_BYPASS_REASON=\"<audit-trail reason>\" to proceed.",
824:            )
825:            _append_telemetry({
826:                "session_id": session_id, "decision": "deny_bypass_no_reason",
827:                "reason": "CODEX_T2_GATE_BYPASS=1 without CODEX_T2_GATE_BYPASS_REASON",
828:                "target_root": str(target_root),
829:            }, target_root)
830:            return 0
831:        _emit_decision(
832:            "warn", "",
833:            additional_context=(
834:                f"[CODEX T2 BYPASS] Reason: {bypass_reason[:200]} | "
835:                f"target: {target_root}. T3 postcommit will still review async."
836:            ),
837:        )
838:        _append_telemetry({
839:            "session_id": session_id, "decision": "bypassed",
840:            "bypass_reason": bypass_reason,
841:            "target_root": str(target_root),
842:        }, target_root)
843:        return 0
844:
845:    in_scope_files = _staged_design_surface_files(target_root, cmd_str)
846:    if not in_scope_files:
847:        _append_telemetry({
848:            "session_id": session_id, "decision": "noop",
849:            "reason": "no design-surface files staged",
850:            "target_root": str(target_root),
851:        }, target_root)
852:        return 0
853:
854:    strict = os.environ.get("CODEX_T2_GATE_STRICT") == "1"
855:    fail_closed = os.environ.get("CODEX_T2_GATE_FAIL_CLOSED") == "1"
856:    # F6: default 120 (was 60 in parent — SOTA's broader allowlist needs more budget)
857:    timeout_sec = int(os.environ.get("CODEX_T2_GATE_TIMEOUT_SEC", "120"))
858:
859:    t2_result = _fire_codex_t2(target_root, timeout_sec)
860:    status = t2_result.get("status", "unknown")
861:    verdict = t2_result.get("verdict")
862:    summary = t2_result.get("summary", "")
863:    findings = t2_result.get("findings", []) or []
864:    findings_n = len(findings) if isinstance(findings, list) else 0
865:
866:    decision = "allow"
867:    reason = ""
868:    additional_context = ""
869:
870:    if status == "ok" and verdict == "approve":
871:        decision = "allow"
872:        reason = f"codex T2 APPROVE — {summary[:200] if summary else ''}"
873:    elif status == "ok" and verdict == "needs-attention":
874:        if strict:
875:            decision = "deny"
876:            reason = (
877:                f"codex T2 NEEDS-ATTENTION ({findings_n} findings): "
878:                f"{summary[:200] if summary else ''} — fix before commit, "
879:                f"OR set CODEX_T2_GATE_STRICT=0 to downgrade to WARN-only"
880:            )
881:        else:
882:            decision = "warn"
883:            additional_context = (
884:                f"[CODEX T2 GATE WARN] verdict=needs-attention, {findings_n} findings. "
885:                f"Summary: {summary[:300] if summary else '(no summary)'}. "
886:                f"Verdict file: {t2_result.get('outfile', '?')}. "
887:                f"Promote to STRICT via CODEX_T2_GATE_STRICT=1. "
888:                f"Per HARD RULE: major SOTA git changes must T2-pass."
889:            )
890:    elif status == "ok" and verdict in ("revert-needed", "revert"):
891:        decision = "deny"
892:        reason = (
893:            f"codex T2 REVERT-NEEDED: {summary[:200] if summary else ''} — "
894:            f"fix and re-commit; STRICT mode required this verdict regardless"
895:        )
896:    elif status == "timeout":
897:        if strict and fail_closed:
898:            decision = "deny"
899:            reason = (
900:                f"codex T2 timeout ({timeout_sec}s) + STRICT+FAIL_CLOSED — "
901:                f"extend with CODEX_T2_GATE_TIMEOUT_SEC=N"
902:            )
903:        else:
904:            decision = "warn"
905:            additional_context = (
906:                f"[CODEX T2 GATE WARN] T2 timed out at {timeout_sec}s. "
907:                f"Proceeding (commit allowed). T3 postcommit will catch missed defects async. "
908:                f"Extend timeout via CODEX_T2_GATE_TIMEOUT_SEC=N."
909:            )
910:    elif status == "cmd_not_found":
911:        if strict and fail_closed:
912:            decision = "deny"
913:            reason = (
914:                "codex T2 cmd_not_found + STRICT+FAIL_CLOSED — "
915:                "install codex CLI or set CODEX_PLUGIN_ROOT"
916:            )
917:        else:
918:            decision = "warn"
919:            additional_context = (
920:                "[CODEX T2 GATE WARN] codex CLI not found; proceeding without T2 review. "
921:                "T3 postcommit hook still catches defects async."
922:            )
923:    else:
924:        # parse_failure, launch_error, unknown — treat as needs-attention semantically
925:        if strict:
926:            decision = "deny"
927:            reason = (
928:                f"codex T2 status={status} (no parseable verdict). "
929:                f"STRICT mode requires a verdict."
930:            )
931:        else:
932:            decision = "warn"
933:            additional_context = (
934:                f"[CODEX T2 GATE WARN] T2 status={status} — no parseable verdict. "
935:                f"Proceeding; T3 postcommit will verify async."
936:            )
937:
938:    _append_telemetry({
939:        "session_id": session_id,
940:        "decision": decision,
941:        "verdict": verdict,
942:        "status": status,
943:        "findings_n": findings_n,
944:        "in_scope_files_n": len(in_scope_files),
945:        "in_scope_files_sample": in_scope_files[:5],

952:        "stderr_tail": (t2_result.get("stderr_tail", "") or "")[:300],
953:    }, target_root)

956:    return 0

163:def _windowless_kwargs() -> dict:
164:    if not _WIN32:
165:        return {}
166:    si = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
167:    si.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
168:    si.wShowWindow = subprocess.SW_HIDE
169:    return {"creationflags": subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW, "startupinfo": si}

377:def _now_iso() -> str:
378:    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
379:
380:
381:def _now_ts() -> float:
382:    return datetime.now(UTC).timestamp()

384:
385:def _normalize(file_path: str) -> str:
386:    if not file_path:
387:        return ""
388:    norm = file_path.replace("\\", "/")
389:    if len(norm) >= 2 and norm[1] == ":":
390:        norm = norm[:2].lower() + norm[2:]
391:    return norm
392:
393:
394:def _to_repo_relpath(norm_path: str) -> str | None:
395:    """Return path relative to _PROJECT_ROOT, POSIX-style, or None if outside.
396:
397:    Used by P0-1 exclusion matching: the exclusion globs are written relative
398:    to repo root (e.g. ".claude/state/**", "data/**"). Matching against the
399:    raw absolute path would never hit; we strip the repo-root prefix first.
400:    """
401:    if not norm_path:
402:        return None
403:    repo_norm = _normalize(str(_PROJECT_ROOT))
404:    if norm_path == repo_norm:
405:        return ""
406:    prefix = repo_norm + "/"
407:    if norm_path.startswith(prefix):
408:        return norm_path[len(prefix):]
409:    return None
410:
411:
412:def _glob_matches(rel_path: str, pattern: str) -> bool:
413:    """Match a relative POSIX path against a glob with ** semantics.
414:
415:    fnmatch.fnmatch alone treats `**` as a single-segment glob. For our
416:    exclusion patterns, `prefix/**` should match every descendant. Strategy:
417:    - If pattern endswith `/**`, also match exact-prefix
418:    - Else fall back to fnmatch
419:    """
420:    if pattern.endswith("/**"):
421:        prefix = pattern[: -len("/**")]
422:        if rel_path == prefix or rel_path.startswith(prefix + "/"):
423:            return True
424:    if "**" in pattern:
425:        # Replace ** with * for fnmatch (fnmatch treats them similarly anyway,
426:        # but explicit conversion avoids surprises with multi-** patterns).
427:        flat = pattern.replace("**", "*")
428:        return fnmatch.fnmatch(rel_path, flat)
429:    return fnmatch.fnmatch(rel_path, pattern)
430:
431:
432:def _path_excluded(target_path: str) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
433:    """P0-1: return (is_excluded, exclusion_reason).
434:
435:    Per codex T1 verdict design_recommendations.P0-1_universal_scope.exclusion_set
436:    + scope:"sss-installed-runtime" + final entry "any resolved path outside the
437:    repo root" (sibling-derived from Z:/claude-sota verdict; _PROJECT_ROOT resolves
438:    runtime root via parents[3] so this hook is sibling-portable per Wave 50 fire 24).
439:    """
440:    norm = _normalize(target_path)
441:    if not norm:
442:        return True, "empty_target_path"
443:
444:    norm_lc = norm.lower()
445:    if norm_lc.startswith(_CROSS_PROJECT_DEPS_PREFIX):
446:        return True, "cross_project_deps_tree"
447:
448:    rel = _to_repo_relpath(norm)
449:    if rel is None:
450:        return True, _OUTSIDE_REPO_EXCLUSION_REASON
451:
452:    # Ship #314.2 precedence guard: ALWAYS-GATED patterns win over
453:    # _UNIVERSAL_EXCLUSIONS. Returning (False, None) here means "this path
454:    # matches a documented gated pattern (sota / audit / feature-handoff
455:    # verdict surfaces); T1 MUST fire regardless of any later exclusion
456:    # match". Closes Ship #314 → #314.1 → #314.2 closed-loop arc per
457:    # .claude/rules/closed-loop-recursive-narrowing.md Round-3 fix-iteration.
458:    for pattern in _ALWAYS_GATED_PATTERNS:
459:        if _glob_matches(rel, pattern):
460:            return False, None
461:
462:    for pattern in _UNIVERSAL_EXCLUSIONS:
463:        if _glob_matches(rel, pattern):
464:            return True, f"glob:{pattern}"
465:    return False, None

470:
471:def _payload_canonical(tool_input: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
472:    """Stable string form of tool_input for SHA-12 fingerprint.
473:
474:    Uses sort_keys=True so order shifts in the dict don't bust the SHA. Strips
475:    keys that vary across retries (none today, but future-proof comment).
476:    """
477:    try:
478:        return json.dumps(tool_input, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
479:    except (TypeError, ValueError):
480:        return str(tool_input)
481:
482:
483:def _sha12(s: str) -> str:
484:    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode("utf-8", errors="replace")).hexdigest()[:12]
485:
486:
487:def _slugify_relpath(rel_path: str | None) -> str:
488:    """Stable, filesystem-safe slug derived from repo-relative path.
489:
490:    Empty/None -> "_root". Replaces / with __ and strips chars not in
491:    [a-zA-Z0-9_.-]. Bounded to 60 chars to keep filenames manageable.
492:    """
493:    if not rel_path:
494:        return "_root"
495:    candidate = rel_path.replace("/", "__")
496:    candidate = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]", "_", candidate)
497:    return candidate[:60] or "_unnamed"

500:def _verdict_paths(target_path: str, tool_input: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[Path, Path, str, str]:
501:    """Compute (prompt_path, out_path, target_sha12, payload_sha12) tuple.
502:
503:    Per codex T1 verdict verdict_persistence_path scheme.
504:    """
505:    norm = _normalize(target_path)
506:    rel = _to_repo_relpath(norm)
507:    slug = _slugify_relpath(rel if rel is not None else norm)
508:    target_sha12 = _sha12(norm)
509:    payload_sha12 = _sha12(_payload_canonical(tool_input))
510:    base = f"codex_consult_auto_t1_{slug}_{target_sha12}_{payload_sha12}"
511:    prompt_path = _STATE_DIR / f"{base}.txt"
512:    out_path = _STATE_DIR / f"{base}_OUT.txt"
513:    return prompt_path, out_path, target_sha12, payload_sha12

520:def _try_acquire_lock(lock_path: Path, spawn_pid: int | None) -> bool:
521:    """Atomically acquire lockfile via O_CREAT|O_EXCL. Returns True if acquired.
522:
523:    Lock contents: JSON {pid, ts, host}. PID is the spawned codex Popen pid
524:    (or 0 if mock/dry-run). Used by retry path to detect stale locks.
525:    """
526:    try:
527:        lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
528:        fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
529:        try:
530:            payload = json.dumps({
531:                "pid": spawn_pid if spawn_pid is not None else 0,
532:                "ts": _now_iso(),
533:                "host": os.environ.get("COMPUTERNAME") or os.environ.get("HOSTNAME") or "",
534:            }).encode("utf-8")
535:            os.write(fd, payload)
536:        finally:
537:            os.close(fd)
538:        return True
539:    except FileExistsError:
540:        return False
541:    except OSError as exc:
542:        log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.lock_acquire:{lock_path.name}")
543:        return False
544:
545:
546:def _release_lock(lock_path: Path) -> None:
547:    try:
548:        lock_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
549:    except OSError as exc:
550:        log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.lock_release:{lock_path.name}")

554:# Per-PreToolUse process dies after each invocation, so bucket state MUST be
555:# durable on-disk (not in-memory). O_CREAT|O_EXCL lock + atomic temp-replace
556:# prevents read-modify-write races across parallel gate invocations.

560:    return ts.astimezone(UTC).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H")

570:
571:def _with_bucket_lock(lock_path: Path, fn) -> bool:
572:    """Execute fn() under O_CREAT|O_EXCL lock with bounded retry.
573:
574:    Returns True if fn() ran (lock acquired), False if gave up after timeout.
575:    """
576:    deadline = time.time() + 5.0
577:    while time.time() < deadline:
578:        try:
579:            lock_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
580:            fd = os.open(str(lock_path), os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
581:            try:
582:                os.write(fd, str(os.getpid()).encode("utf-8"))
583:            finally:
584:                os.close(fd)
585:            try:
586:                fn()
587:            finally:
588:                try:
589:                    lock_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
590:                except OSError as exc:
591:                    log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.bucket_lock_release:{lock_path.name}")
592:            return True
593:        except FileExistsError:
594:            time.sleep(0.05)
595:        except OSError as exc:
596:            log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.bucket_lock_acquire:{lock_path.name}")
597:            return False
598:    return False

601:def _empty_pending_bucket(date_hour: str) -> dict:
602:    return {
603:        "schema": "ct1cg.v2.bucket.pending",
604:        "date_hour": date_hour,
605:        "decision": "allow_excluded",
606:        "first_ts": None,
607:        "last_ts": None,
608:        "entries": [],
609:    }
610:
611:
612:def _load_pending_bucket(path: Path, date_hour: str) -> dict:
613:    if not path.is_file():
614:        return _empty_pending_bucket(date_hour)
615:    try:
616:        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as fh:
617:            data = json.load(fh)
618:        if isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("entries"), list):
619:            return data
620:    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
621:        log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.bucket_load:{path.name}")
622:    return _empty_pending_bucket(date_hour)
623:
624:
625:def _write_pending_bucket_atomic(path: Path, bucket: dict) -> None:
626:    """Atomic temp-write + os.replace prevents partial-write on crash mid-flush."""
627:    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
628:    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
629:    try:
630:        with tmp.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
631:            json.dump(bucket, fh, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
632:        os.replace(str(tmp), str(path))
633:    except OSError as exc:
634:        log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.bucket_write:{path.name}")
635:        try:
636:            tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
637:        except OSError:
638:            pass
639:
640:
641:def _increment_allow_excluded_bucket(bucket: dict, entry: dict) -> dict:
642:    """Find matching entry by full-tuple key (codex T1 F2: include agent_id+agent_type)."""
643:    key_fields = (
644:        "target_path", "tool_name", "exclusion_reason",
645:        "session_id", "agent_id", "agent_type",
646:    )
647:    new_key = tuple(entry.get(k) for k in key_fields)
648:    ts = entry["ts"]
649:    bucket["last_ts"] = ts
650:    if bucket["first_ts"] is None:
651:        bucket["first_ts"] = ts
652:    for existing in bucket["entries"]:
653:        if tuple(existing.get(k) for k in key_fields) == new_key:
654:            existing["count"] = existing.get("count", 0) + 1
655:            existing["last_ts"] = ts
656:            return bucket
657:    new_entry = {k: entry.get(k) for k in key_fields}
658:    new_entry["count"] = 1
659:    new_entry["first_ts"] = ts
660:    new_entry["last_ts"] = ts
661:    bucket["entries"].append(new_entry)
662:    return bucket
663:
664:
665:def _flush_allow_excluded_bucket_if_due(date_hour: str, now_ts: float) -> None:
666:    """Flush pending bucket → JSONL row if older than _FLUSH_INTERVAL_SEC.
667:
668:    Idempotent (no-op when bucket missing / not yet due / lock contended).
669:    """
670:    pending_path = _bucket_pending_path(date_hour)
671:    if not pending_path.is_file():
672:        return
673:    try:
674:        mtime = pending_path.stat().st_mtime
675:    except OSError:
676:        return
677:    if now_ts - mtime < _ALLOW_EXCLUDED_BUCKET_FLUSH_INTERVAL_SEC:
678:        return
679:    lock_path = _bucket_lock_path(date_hour)
680:
681:    def _do_flush():
682:        if not pending_path.is_file():
683:            return
684:        try:
685:            with pending_path.open(encoding="utf-8") as fh:
686:                bucket = json.load(fh)
687:        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
688:            log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.bucket_flush_load:{pending_path.name}")
689:            return
690:        flushed = {
691:            "schema": _TELEMETRY_SCHEMA_BUCKET,
692:            "ts": datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
693:            "date_hour": bucket.get("date_hour"),
694:            "decision": "allow_excluded",
695:            "first_ts": bucket.get("first_ts"),
696:            "last_ts": bucket.get("last_ts"),
697:            "entries": bucket.get("entries", []),
698:        }
699:        try:
700:            _TELEMETRY_LOG.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
701:            with _TELEMETRY_LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
702:                fh.write(json.dumps(flushed, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
703:            pending_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
704:        except OSError as exc:
705:            log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.bucket_flush_write:{pending_path.name}")
706:
707:    _with_bucket_lock(lock_path, _do_flush)
708:
709:
710:def _record_allow_excluded_bucket(
711:    *,
712:    event: dict,
713:    session_id: str,
714:    target_path: str,
715:    tool_name: str,
716:    exclusion_reason: str | None,
717:) -> None:
718:    """Update on-disk pending bucket atomically; never write to _TELEMETRY_LOG inline.
719:
720:    Per codex T1 F1 P1: replaces the prior in-memory + Stop-hook flush design that
721:    was unsafe because the gate is a per-PreToolUse subprocess (memory dies after
722:    each invocation). Bucket entries dedup by 6-tuple key including agent_id +
723:    agent_type so parallel-agent telemetry attribution is preserved (F2 P1).
724:    """
725:    now = datetime.now(UTC)
726:    date_hour = _date_hour(now)
727:    pending_path = _bucket_pending_path(date_hour)
728:    lock_path = _bucket_lock_path(date_hour)
729:    agent_id = event.get("agent_id") if isinstance(event, dict) else None
730:    agent_type = event.get("agent_type") if isinstance(event, dict) else None
731:    entry = {
732:        "ts": now.isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
733:        "target_path": target_path,
734:        "tool_name": tool_name,
735:        "exclusion_reason": exclusion_reason,
736:        "session_id": session_id,
737:        "agent_id": agent_id,
738:        "agent_type": agent_type,
739:    }
740:
741:    def _do_update():
742:        bucket = _load_pending_bucket(pending_path, date_hour)
743:        bucket = _increment_allow_excluded_bucket(bucket, entry)
744:        _write_pending_bucket_atomic(pending_path, bucket)
745:
746:    _with_bucket_lock(lock_path, _do_update)
747:    # Optimistic flush — idempotent if not due (no-op when fresh OR lock contended).
748:    _flush_allow_excluded_bucket_if_due(date_hour, time.time())
749:
750:
751:def _lock_age_sec(lock_path: Path) -> float | None:
752:    try:
753:        return _now_ts() - lock_path.stat().st_mtime
754:    except OSError:
755:        return None

758:def _auto_state_append(row: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
759:    """Append-only event log for P0-2 auto-spawn state reconstruction.
760:
761:    Pattern source: codex_review_queue.py:30-50 (append-only events;
762:    state per dedupe_key reconstructed by walking log). Per codex T1
763:    verdict failure_classification.telemetry_write_error: fail_open only
764:    for excluded targets; we always attempt and log failures to stderr.
765:    """
766:    row.setdefault("ts", _now_iso())
767:    try:
768:        _AUTO_T1_STATE_LOG.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
769:        with _AUTO_T1_STATE_LOG.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
770:            f.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
771:    except OSError as exc:
772:        log_swallow(exc, "codex_t1_gate.auto_state_append")

777:
778:def _classify_sensitive(target_path: str, tool_input: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
779:    """Return (is_blocked, reason_id). Per codex T1 verdict failure_classification:
780:    blocked_sensitive => do NOT write prompt containing payload, do NOT spawn codex.
781:
782:    Uses sibling codex_gate classifiers when importable (loose-coupled).
783:    Inline fallback rules cover the most-critical patterns if codex_gate moves.
784:    """
785:    paths_to_classify = [target_path]
786:    # Also classify referenced paths inside tool_input (e.g. MultiEdit edits each
787:    # have a file_path subkey). Keep cheap — limit to top-level dict scan.
788:    if isinstance(tool_input, dict):
789:        for k in ("file_path", "path", "notebook_path"):
790:            v = tool_input.get(k)
791:            if isinstance(v, str) and v:
792:                paths_to_classify.append(v)
793:    if _gate_classify_paths is not None:
794:        ok, reason = _gate_classify_paths(paths_to_classify)
795:        if not ok:
796:            return True, reason
797:    else:  # inline minimal fallback
798:        for p in paths_to_classify:
799:            pn = _normalize(p)
800:            if re.search(r"(^|/)(secrets/|credentials/|\.aws/|\.ssh/|auth/)", pn, re.IGNORECASE):
801:                return True, f"forbidden_path:{pn[:80]}"
802:            if re.search(r"(^|/)\.env(\..*)?$", pn, re.IGNORECASE):
803:                return True, f"forbidden_path:{pn[:80]}"
804:            if re.search(r"\.(pem|key)$", pn, re.IGNORECASE):
805:                return True, f"forbidden_path:{pn[:80]}"
806:
807:    # Content classification: scan proposed Edit/Write content for secret patterns
808:    content_blob = ""
809:    if isinstance(tool_input, dict):
810:        for k in ("content", "new_string", "new_content"):
811:            v = tool_input.get(k)
812:            if isinstance(v, str):
813:                content_blob += "\n" + v
814:        edits = tool_input.get("edits")
815:        if isinstance(edits, list):
816:            for ed in edits:
817:                if isinstance(ed, dict):
818:                    ns = ed.get("new_string")
819:                    if isinstance(ns, str):
820:                        content_blob += "\n" + ns
821:    if _gate_classify_content is not None and content_blob:
822:        ok, reason = _gate_classify_content(content_blob)
823:        if not ok:
824:            return True, reason
825:    return False, None

830:
831:def _git_status_short(cwd: Path, max_bytes: int = 4096) -> str:
832:    git_exe = shutil.which("git")
833:    if git_exe is None:
834:        return "(git not on PATH)"
835:    try:
836:        result = subprocess.run(
837:            [git_exe, "status", "--short"],
838:            cwd=str(cwd), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
839:            **_windowless_kwargs(),
840:        )
841:        out = result.stdout if result.returncode == 0 else result.stderr
842:        return (out or "")[:max_bytes]
843:    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
844:        return f"(git status failed: {exc!r}[:120])"
845:
846:
847:def _git_diff_target(cwd: Path, target_rel: str | None, max_bytes: int = 8192) -> str:
848:    if target_rel is None:
849:        return "(target not in repo; no diff applicable)"
850:    git_exe = shutil.which("git")
851:    if git_exe is None:
852:        return ""
853:    try:
854:        result = subprocess.run(
855:            [git_exe, "diff", "--", target_rel],
856:            cwd=str(cwd), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
857:            **_windowless_kwargs(),
858:        )
859:        return (result.stdout or "")[:max_bytes]
860:    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
861:        return ""
862:
863:
864:def _read_current_file_context(target_path: str, max_bytes: int = 4096) -> str:
865:    p = Path(target_path)
866:    if not p.is_file():
867:        return "(missing-file marker — Write would create new)"
868:    try:
869:        return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:max_bytes]
870:    except OSError as exc:
871:        return f"(read error: {exc!r}[:120])"
872:
873:
874:def _nearest_instructions(target_path: str, max_bytes: int = 2048) -> str:
875:    """Find-up nearest CLAUDE.md from target dir up to repo root."""
876:    try:
877:        p = Path(target_path).resolve().parent
878:    except OSError:
879:        return "(could not resolve parent of target)"
880:    repo_root = _PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
881:    while True:
882:        candidate = p / "CLAUDE.md"
883:        if candidate.is_file():
884:            try:
885:                return f"{candidate}:\n" + candidate.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:max_bytes]
886:            except OSError:
887:                pass
888:        if p == repo_root or p == p.parent:
889:            break
890:        p = p.parent
891:    return "(no CLAUDE.md found between target and repo root)"

893:
894:def _build_prompt(
895:    target_path: str,
896:    tool_input: dict[str, Any],
897:    tool_name: str,
898:    session_id: str,
899:    exclusion_verdict: str,
900:) -> str:
901:    """Build the codex T1 prompt per verdict prompt_template.
902:
903:    Structure: SYSTEM block + CONTEXT block + decision-rule block. Codex T1
904:    verdict explicit (item_66.design_recommendations.P0-2_auto_spawn.prompt_template).
905:    """
906:    norm = _normalize(target_path)
907:    rel = _to_repo_relpath(norm) or norm
908:    cwd = _PROJECT_ROOT
909:    git_status = _git_status_short(cwd)
910:    git_diff = _git_diff_target(cwd, rel)
911:    cur_ctx = _read_current_file_context(target_path)
912:    instructions = _nearest_instructions(target_path)
913:    proposed = _payload_canonical(tool_input)
914:    if len(proposed) > 16384:
915:        proposed_sha = _sha12(proposed)
916:        proposed = (
917:            f"<LARGE-PAYLOAD len={len(proposed)} sha12={proposed_sha} "
918:            f"head_first_2048>\n{proposed[:2048]}\n<...truncated...>\n"
919:            f"<tail_last_2048>\n{proposed[-2048:]}"
920:        )
921:    return (
922:        "SYSTEM: You are Codex GPT-5.5 acting as the sss STRICT T1 pre-edit reviewer. "
923:        "Review the proposed tool operation before it is applied. Do not modify files. "
924:        "Return strict JSON only on the LAST line: "
925:        '{"verdict":"APPROVE|NEEDS-REVISION|REVERT","confidence":0.0,'
926:        '"findings":[{"severity":"critical|high|medium|low","file":"...","line_start":1,'
927:        '"line_end":1,"title":"...","body":"...","recommendation":"..."}],'
928:        '"summary":"...","next_steps":["..."]}. '
929:        "APPROVE only if the exact proposed Edit/Write/MultiEdit is safe, "
930:        "repo-rule compliant, non-secret-leaking, and does not need a different design.\n\n"
931:        "CONTEXT:\n"
932:        f"- repo_root: {_PROJECT_ROOT}\n"
933:        f"- target_path: {norm}\n"
934:        f"- tool_name: {tool_name}\n"
935:        f"- session_id: {session_id}\n"
936:        f"- exclusion_verdict: {exclusion_verdict}\n"
937:        f"\n--- git_status ---\n{git_status}\n"
938:        f"\n--- nearest_instructions ---\n{instructions}\n"
939:        f"\n--- current_file_context (first {len(cur_ctx)} bytes) ---\n{cur_ctx}\n"
940:        f"\n--- proposed_operation ---\n{proposed}\n"
941:        f"\n--- relevant_diff ---\n{git_diff}\n"
942:        "\nDecision rule for sss STRICT gate: APPROVE permits retry; "
943:        "NEEDS-REVISION, REVERT, timeout, parse_failure, or missing JSON denies.\n"
944:    )

950:def _resolve_codex_argv0() -> list[str] | None:
951:    """Mirror of codex_t2_pre_commit_gate._resolve_codex_argv0 (sibling 282-296).
952:
953:    Per cycle-479 BYPASS-SHIM: prefer node.exe + codex.js direct path over
954:    codex.exe wrapper for stdin reliability on Windows detached subprocess.
955:    """
956:    plugin_root_env = os.environ.get("CODEX_PLUGIN_ROOT")
957:    if plugin_root_env:
958:        codex_js = Path(plugin_root_env) / "codex.js"
959:        if codex_js.is_file():
960:            node = shutil.which("node") or shutil.which("node.exe")
961:            if node:
962:                return [node, str(codex_js)]
963:    codex = shutil.which("codex") or shutil.which("codex.cmd") or shutil.which("codex.exe")
964:    if codex:
965:        return [codex]
966:    return None

969:def _spawn_codex_async(
970:    prompt_text: str,
971:    prompt_path: Path,
972:    out_path: Path,
973:    target_sha12: str,
974:    payload_sha12: str,
975:    timeout_sec: int,
976:) -> dict[str, Any]:
977:    """Spawn codex max-unleash review of prompt; return spawn metadata.
978:
979:    Mock short-circuit: env CODEX_T1_AUTO_SPAWN_MOCK={approve|needs-revision|revert|skip}
980:    writes synthetic verdict to out_path immediately + returns mock pid.
981:
982:    Returns {pid, status, codex_argv, codex_home, mock} where status is one of:
983:      ok_started, mock_written, cmd_not_found, launch_error, mock_skip
984:    """
985:    mock = os.environ.get("CODEX_T1_AUTO_SPAWN_MOCK")
986:    if mock:
987:        if mock == "skip":
988:            return {"pid": 0, "status": "mock_skip", "mock": "skip"}
989:        verdict_map = {
990:            "approve": "APPROVE",
991:            "needs-revision": "NEEDS-REVISION",
992:            "revert": "REVERT",
993:        }
994:        verdict = verdict_map.get(mock, "APPROVE")
995:        synthetic = json.dumps({
996:            "verdict": verdict,
997:            "confidence": 0.99,
998:            "findings": [],
999:            "summary": f"MOCK auto-T1 verdict={verdict}",
1000:            "next_steps": [],
1001:        })
1002:        try:
1003:            out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1004:            out_path.write_text(synthetic + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
1005:        except OSError as exc:
1006:            return {"pid": 0, "status": "launch_error", "error": f"mock_write:{exc!r}"}
1007:        return {"pid": 0, "status": "mock_written", "mock": mock}
1008:
1009:    codex_argv0 = _resolve_codex_argv0()
1010:    if codex_argv0 is None:
1011:        return {"pid": 0, "status": "cmd_not_found"}
1012:
1013:    # Persist prompt to disk so codex can read via stdin file-redirect
1014:    try:
1015:        prompt_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1016:        prompt_path.write_text(prompt_text, encoding="utf-8")
1017:    except OSError as exc:
1018:        return {"pid": 0, "status": "launch_error", "error": f"prompt_write:{exc!r}"}
1019:
1020:    # CODEX_HOME isolation per codex T1 verdict (sibling pattern at
1021:    # codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:332-333). _PROJECT_ROOT is the runtime repo
1022:    # root (resolves dynamically via parents[3] per port-note-discipline §2
1023:    # Discipline 1; portable across sibling claude-sota and installed runtime).
1024:    # Honor ambient CODEX_HOME (set by tools/eee.ps1 to state-outside-repo
1025:    # Z:/claude-sota-installed-state/.codex per CLAUDE.local.md (e) state-outside-repo
1026:    # discipline) when present; fall back to repo-root .codex otherwise. Wave 50 fire 35
1027:    # P1-2 fix-forward per Agent JJ adversarial review at
1028:    # tmp/wave50-fire35-agentJJ-installed-runtime-adversarial-2026-05-06.md (codex auth
1029:    # was migrated state-outside-repo but hook hardcoded repo-root override broke T1-T7
1030:    # silently in installed runtime).
1031:    codex_home = Path(os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME", str(_PROJECT_ROOT / ".codex")))
1032:    spawn_env = {**os.environ, "CODEX_HOME": str(codex_home)}
1033:
1034:    cmd: list[str] = [
1035:        *codex_argv0,
1036:        "exec",
1037:        # `--ignore-rules` added 2026-04-30 per codex T1 NEEDS-REVISION verdict
1038:        # (.claude/state/codex_consult_sota_synthesis_2026_04_30_OUT.txt). Skips
1039:        # user/project execpolicy `.rules` files for harness isolation.
1040:        # Cite: Z:/repos/deps/codex/codex-rs/exec/src/cli.rs:35 @ HEAD 993e3f407.
1041:        # NOT adding `--ignore-user-config` (codex T1 HIGH finding): would skip
1042:        # $CODEX_HOME/config.toml, breaking the `-p deep-review-exec` profile this
1043:        # hook depends on (CODEX_HOME=.codex set at line 750 above).
1044:        "--ignore-rules",
1045:        "--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox",
1046:        "-c", "sandbox_workspace_write.network_access=true",
1047:        "-c", 'web_search="live"',
1048:        "-c", 'model_reasoning_effort="xhigh"',
1049:        "-c", 'model_reasoning_summary="detailed"',
1050:        "-p", "deep-review-exec",
1051:        "-C", str(_PROJECT_ROOT),
1052:        "--json",
1053:        "--ephemeral",
1054:        "--color", "never",
1055:        "--output-last-message", str(out_path),
1056:        "-",  # read prompt from stdin
1057:    ]
1058:
1059:    # Windows console-suppression mirroring T2 sibling pattern (line 379-392).
1060:    creationflags = 0
1061:    startupinfo = None
1062:    if sys.platform == "win32":
1063:        creationflags = 0x10  # CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE (HIDDEN via SW_HIDE below)
1064:        startupinfo = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
1065:        startupinfo.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
1066:        startupinfo.wShowWindow = subprocess.SW_HIDE
1067:
1068:    # fire-18 SUPERVISOR REFACTOR (per fire-17 T1 NEEDS-REVISION 0.86 + T5/T0 chain):
1069:    # Spawn DETACHED Python supervisor (scripts/_codex_jsonl_runner.py) instead of
1070:    # codex directly. Supervisor opens prompt-path itself, runs codex with --json,
1071:    # parses turn.completed events, captures stderr concurrently, writes atomic
1072:    # sidecar JSON on completion/timeout. Hook returns ≤5s; supervisor runs detached
1073:    # for up to timeout_sec. Closes Pattern B HNF zero-investigation subclass (n=8+
1074:    # ladder per codex-t1-fix-forward-pattern.md §Pattern-B mitigation patterns).
1075:    sidecar_path = _runner_sidecar_path(out_path)
1076:    runner_path = _PROJECT_ROOT / "scripts" / "_codex_jsonl_runner.py"
1077:    runner_argv = [
1078:        sys.executable,
1079:        str(runner_path),
1080:        "--prompt-path", str(prompt_path),
1081:        "--codex-argv", json.dumps(cmd),
1082:        "--out-path", str(out_path),
1083:        "--sidecar-path", str(sidecar_path),
1084:        "--timeout-sec", str(timeout_sec),
1085:    ]
1086:    try:
1087:        proc = subprocess.Popen(
1088:            runner_argv,
1089:            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,    # supervisor opens prompt_path itself
1090:            stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,   # supervisor writes to sidecar_path
1091:            stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
1092:            cwd=str(_PROJECT_ROOT),
1093:            env=spawn_env,                # CODEX_HOME preserved
1094:            creationflags=creationflags,
1095:            startupinfo=startupinfo,
1096:        )
1097:        return {
1098:            "pid": proc.pid,             # NOTE: now SUPERVISOR pid, not codex pid
1099:            "runner_pid": proc.pid,      # NEW (fire-18) — telemetry
1100:            "status": "ok_started",
1101:            "codex_argv": codex_argv0,
1102:            "codex_home": str(codex_home),
1103:            "timeout_sec": timeout_sec,
1104:            "sidecar_path": str(sidecar_path),  # NEW (fire-18) — downstream parser key
1105:        }
1106:    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
1107:        return {"pid": 0, "status": "launch_error", "error": repr(exc)[:200]}

1116:def _runner_sidecar_path(out_path: Path) -> Path:
1117:    """Single source of truth for sidecar naming (per fire-17 T5/T1 phase-2.5 insert #1).
1118:
1119:    Used by both _spawn_codex_async() and _parse_t1_verdict() to prevent
1120:    disagreement on the <out_path>.runner.json sidecar shape produced by

1125:
1126:def _classify_parse_failure(raw_output: str, stderr_tail: str) -> str:
1127:    """T1-side mirror of T2's _classify_parse_failure (per fire-17 T1 P1#3+P1#8).
1128:
1129:    7-class subclass vocabulary copied verbatim from
1130:    codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:180-228 to harmonize T1+T2 telemetry.
1131:    """
1132:    s = stderr_tail or ""
1133:    r = (raw_output or "").strip()
1134:    if not r:
1135:        if "unexpected argument" in s or "Usage:" in s:
1136:            return "parse_failure_cli_argv_rejected"
1137:        if "error:" in s.lower():
1138:            return "parse_failure_cli_error"
1139:        return "parse_failure_empty_output"
1140:    if not (r.startswith("{") or r.startswith("[")):
1141:        return "parse_failure_no_json_block"
1142:    try:
1143:        data = json.loads(r)
1144:    except json.JSONDecodeError:
1145:        return "parse_failure_malformed_json"
1146:    if not isinstance(data, dict):
1147:        return "parse_failure_non_dict_root"
1148:    if "verdict" not in data:
1149:        return "parse_failure_missing_verdict_key"
1150:    return "parse_failure_unknown"
