1152:
1153:def _parse_t1_verdict(out_path: Path) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
1154:    """Return (verdict, classification).
1155:
1156:    15-class harmonized vocabulary (per fire-17 T1 P1#6 corrected from stale '12 total'):
1157:      - APPROVE / NEEDS-REVISION / REVERT (T1 verdict shape)
1158:      - auth_not_logged_in / network_down_or_rate_limit / sandbox_or_profile_config_error
1159:      - timeout_124_codex_stalled (NEW gstack: timeout_detected OR timeout_kind=='rc_124' OR rc==124)
1160:      - parse_failure_zero_turn_disconnect (NEW gstack: rc=0 AND turn_completed_count==0)
1161:      - parse_failure_cli_argv_rejected / parse_failure_cli_error /
1162:        parse_failure_empty_output / parse_failure_no_json_block /
1163:        parse_failure_malformed_json / parse_failure_non_dict_root /
1164:        parse_failure_missing_verdict_key (T2-mirror, 7 classes)
1165:
1166:    Parser stage order (per fire-17 T1 P1 prescriptions):
1167:      1. Sidecar terminal: timeout 3-way OR / auth_status / rc=0 zero-turn (P1#5+P1#1)
1168:      2. Out_path raw auth/rate/sandbox patterns (UNCHANGED)
1169:      3. Strict JSON verdict parse (UNCHANGED)
1170:      4. Regex fallback (UNCHANGED)
1171:      5. T2-mirror parse-failure subclass (passes sidecar_stderr_tail per P1#1)
1172:
1173:    Sidecar tolerance (per P1#2): non-dict sidecar JSON falls through gracefully
1174:    via isinstance check + ValueError catch.
1175:    """
1176:    sidecar_path = _runner_sidecar_path(out_path)
1177:    sidecar_stderr_tail = ""
1178:
1179:    # Stage 1: sidecar terminal classifications
1180:    if sidecar_path.is_file():
1181:        try:
1182:            sidecar = json.loads(sidecar_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
1183:            if not isinstance(sidecar, dict):
1184:                raise ValueError("sidecar root must be JSON object")
1185:            sidecar_stderr_tail = sidecar.get("stderr_tail", "") or ""
1186:            # Timeout 3-way OR (per T1 P1#5: rc=124 must classify timeout
1187:            # even without timeout_detected flag set)
1188:            if (
1189:                sidecar.get("timeout_detected")
1190:                or sidecar.get("timeout_kind") == "rc_124"
1191:                or sidecar.get("returncode") == 124
1192:            ):
1193:                return None, "timeout_124_codex_stalled"
1194:            auth = sidecar.get("auth_status", "ok")
1195:            if auth == "auth_failed":
1196:                return None, "auth_not_logged_in"
1197:            if auth == "rate_limit_429":
1198:                return None, "network_down_or_rate_limit"
1199:            if sidecar.get("returncode") == 0 and sidecar.get("turn_completed_count", -1) == 0:
1200:                return None, "parse_failure_zero_turn_disconnect"
1201:        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, ValueError) as exc:
1202:            log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.sidecar_read:{sidecar_path.name}")
1203:            # tolerate as non-terminal — fall through with sidecar_stderr_tail=""
1204:
1205:    # Stage 2: existing out_path raw parse (UNCHANGED logic, classes harmonized)
1206:    if not out_path.is_file():
1207:        return None, _classify_parse_failure("", sidecar_stderr_tail)
1208:    try:
1209:        raw = out_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:_OUTPUT_READ_BYTES]
1210:    except OSError as exc:
1211:        log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.verdict_read:{out_path.name}")
1212:        return None, _classify_parse_failure("", sidecar_stderr_tail)
1213:    if not raw.strip():
1214:        return None, _classify_parse_failure("", sidecar_stderr_tail)
1215:
1216:    raw_lc = raw.lower()
1217:    # Diagnose non-verdict failure shapes
1218:    if "not logged in" in raw_lc or "auth.json" in raw_lc and "missing" in raw_lc:
1219:        return None, "auth_not_logged_in"
1220:    if "rate limit" in raw_lc or "429" in raw_lc or "network unreachable" in raw_lc:
1221:        return None, "network_down_or_rate_limit"
1222:    if "sandbox" in raw_lc and ("denied" in raw_lc or "forbidden" in raw_lc):
1223:        return None, "sandbox_or_profile_config_error"
1224:
1225:    # Strict JSON parse first (codex --output-last-message writes the last
1226:    # agent message which is typically the JSON verdict alone).
1227:    try:
1228:        for line in reversed([ln for ln in raw.splitlines() if ln.strip()]):
1229:            try:
1230:                obj = json.loads(line)
1231:                if isinstance(obj, dict) and "verdict" in obj:
1232:                    v = str(obj.get("verdict", "")).upper()
1233:                    if v in {"APPROVE", "NEEDS-REVISION", "REVERT"}:
1234:                        return v, v
1235:            except json.JSONDecodeError:
1236:                continue
1237:    except (OSError, ValueError):
1238:        pass
1239:
1240:    # Regex fallback — verdict embedded inside a larger response
1241:    m = _VERDICT_RE.search(raw)
1242:    if m:
1243:        v = m.group(1).upper()
1244:        return v, v
1245:
1246:    # T2-mirror parse-failure subclass (per fire-17 T1 P1#1: pass sidecar_stderr_tail
1247:    # for accurate parse_failure_cli_argv_rejected / parse_failure_cli_error detection)
1248:    return None, _classify_parse_failure(raw, sidecar_stderr_tail)
1249:

1254:def _auto_spawn_codex_t1(
1255:    target_path: str,
1256:    tool_input: dict[str, Any],
1257:    tool_name: str,
1258:    session_id: str,
1259:    exclusion_verdict: str,
1260:    timeout_sec: int,
1261:) -> dict[str, Any]:
1262:    """Top-level auto-spawn orchestrator. Returns dict with classification fields:
1263:
1264:    {
1265:      "classification": one of the 11 codex T1 verdict failure_classification keys,
1266:      "verdict": "APPROVE"|"NEEDS-REVISION"|"REVERT"|None,
1267:      "decision": "allow"|"deny",
1268:      "deny_reason": str|None,
1269:      "in_progress": bool,
1270:      ... metadata fields ...
1271:    }
1272:
1273:    Decision logic:
1274:    - Existing OUT for (target_sha12, payload_sha12) → parse + classify
1275:    - Lockfile present + fresh → in_progress=True, deny "AUTO-T1 IN PROGRESS"
1276:    - Lockfile present + stale → release + retry spawn
1277:    - Sensitive payload → blocked_sensitive, deny, do NOT spawn
1278:    - No lock + no verdict → acquire lock + spawn + deny "AUTO-T1 IN PROGRESS"
1279:    """
1280:    prompt_path, out_path, target_sha12, payload_sha12 = _verdict_paths(target_path, tool_input)
1281:    lock_path = _lockfile_path(target_sha12, payload_sha12)
1282:
1283:    base_meta: dict[str, Any] = {
1284:        "target_path": _normalize(target_path),
1285:        "target_sha12": target_sha12,
1286:        "payload_sha12": payload_sha12,
1287:        "tool_name": tool_name,
1288:        "session_id": session_id,
1289:        "exclusion_verdict": exclusion_verdict,
1290:        "out_path": str(out_path),
1291:        "lock_path": str(lock_path),
1292:    }
1293:
1294:    # Phase A: existing verdict file OR sidecar already on disk → reuse it
1295:    # (fire-18 SIDECAR-OR-OUT-PATH check per fire-17 T5/T1 phase-2.5 insert #5 —
1296:    #  sidecar-only outcomes like zero-turn / timeout / auth-fail must classify
1297:    #  even when out_path absent; supervisor at scripts/_codex_jsonl_runner.py
1298:    #  may write only sidecar in those cases.)
1299:    sidecar_path_phase_a = _runner_sidecar_path(out_path)
1300:    sidecar_exists = sidecar_path_phase_a.is_file()
1301:    out_path_ready = out_path.is_file() and out_path.stat().st_size > 0
1302:    if sidecar_exists or out_path_ready:
1303:        verdict, classification = _parse_t1_verdict(out_path)
1304:        # If a verdict landed, lockfile (if any) is now stale → clean up
1305:        _release_lock(lock_path)
1306:        result: dict[str, Any] = {**base_meta, "classification": classification, "verdict": verdict}
1307:        if verdict == "APPROVE":
1308:            result["decision"] = "allow"
1309:            result["deny_reason"] = None
1310:        else:
1311:            result["decision"] = "deny"
1312:            result["deny_reason"] = (
1313:                f"codex auto-T1 verdict={verdict or '(unparseable)'} "
1314:                f"classification={classification}; see {out_path.name}"
1315:                + (f" + sidecar={sidecar_path_phase_a.name}" if sidecar_exists else "")
1316:            )
1317:        result["in_progress"] = False
1318:        _auto_state_append({**result, "event": "resolved"})
1319:        return result
1320:
1321:    # Phase B: lockfile present → check freshness
1322:    if lock_path.exists():
1323:        age = _lock_age_sec(lock_path)
1324:        # Stale lock = age > timeout + grace → release + fall through to re-spawn
1325:        if age is not None and age > (timeout_sec + _LOCKFILE_STALE_GRACE_SEC):
1326:            _release_lock(lock_path)
1327:            _auto_state_append({**base_meta, "event": "stale_lock_released", "age_sec": round(age, 2)})
1328:        else:
1329:            # Fresh lock — codex still working
1330:            in_prog = {
1331:                **base_meta, "classification": "in_progress",
1332:                "verdict": None, "decision": "deny",
1333:                "deny_reason": (
1334:                    f"AUTO-T1 IN PROGRESS for {target_path} "
1335:                    f"(target_sha12={target_sha12}, payload_sha12={payload_sha12}). "
1336:                    f"Retry the same Edit in ~30s. Verdict will land at {out_path.name}. "
1337:                    f"Lockfile age={age:.1f}s, timeout={timeout_sec}s."
1338:                ),
1339:                "in_progress": True,
1340:            }
1341:            _auto_state_append({**in_prog, "event": "in_progress_seen"})
1342:            return in_prog
1343:
1344:    # Phase C: no lock + no verdict → sensitivity gate then spawn
1345:    blocked, sensitivity_reason = _classify_sensitive(target_path, tool_input)
1346:    if blocked:
1347:        result_blocked = {
1348:            **base_meta, "classification": "blocked_sensitive",
1349:            "verdict": None, "decision": "deny",
1350:            "deny_reason": (
1351:                f"BLOCKED_SENSITIVE for {target_path}: {sensitivity_reason}. "
1352:                f"Prompt was NOT written; codex was NOT spawned. "
1353:                f"Per-edit bypass UNSUPPORTED (Ship #312 / T3 R3 0.97 ba04d86), "
1354:                f"or remediate the sensitive content/path and retry."
1355:            ),
1356:            "in_progress": False,
1357:            "sensitivity_reason": sensitivity_reason,
1358:        }
1359:        _auto_state_append({**result_blocked, "event": "blocked_sensitive"})
1360:        return result_blocked
1361:
1362:    # Acquire lock (atomic) — handles race where another hook fired same target
1363:    if not _try_acquire_lock(lock_path, spawn_pid=None):
1364:        # Race: someone else just acquired it. Treat as in_progress.
1365:        in_prog_race = {
1366:            **base_meta, "classification": "in_progress",
1367:            "verdict": None, "decision": "deny",
1368:            "deny_reason": (
1369:                f"AUTO-T1 IN PROGRESS (lock race) for {target_path}. "
1370:                f"Retry in ~30s. Verdict at {out_path.name}."
1371:            ),
1372:            "in_progress": True,
1373:        }
1374:        _auto_state_append({**in_prog_race, "event": "lock_race"})
1375:        return in_prog_race
1376:
1377:    # Build prompt + spawn codex
1378:    try:
1379:        prompt_text = _build_prompt(target_path, tool_input, tool_name, session_id, exclusion_verdict)
1380:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open by releasing lock + classifying
1381:        log_swallow(exc, "codex_t1_gate.prompt_build")
1382:        _release_lock(lock_path)
1383:        result_pe = {
1384:            **base_meta, "classification": "sandbox_or_profile_config_error",
1385:            "verdict": None, "decision": "deny",
1386:            "deny_reason": f"prompt build failed: {type(exc).__name__}",
1387:            "in_progress": False,
1388:        }
1389:        _auto_state_append({**result_pe, "event": "prompt_build_failed"})
1390:        return result_pe
1391:
1392:    spawn_meta = _spawn_codex_async(
1393:        prompt_text, prompt_path, out_path, target_sha12, payload_sha12, timeout_sec,
1394:    )
1395:    spawn_status = spawn_meta.get("status", "launch_error")
1396:
1397:    # Mock paths: synthesize verdict immediately, treat as resolved
1398:    if spawn_status == "mock_written":
1399:        _release_lock(lock_path)
1400:        verdict, classification = _parse_t1_verdict(out_path)
1401:        result_mock: dict[str, Any] = {
1402:            **base_meta, "classification": classification,
1403:            "verdict": verdict, "mock": spawn_meta.get("mock"),
1404:        }
1405:        if verdict == "APPROVE":
1406:            result_mock["decision"] = "allow"
1407:            result_mock["deny_reason"] = None
1408:        else:
1409:            result_mock["decision"] = "deny"
1410:            result_mock["deny_reason"] = (
1411:                f"MOCK auto-T1 verdict={verdict} classification={classification}"
1412:            )
1413:        result_mock["in_progress"] = False
1414:        _auto_state_append({**result_mock, "event": "mock_resolved"})
1415:        return result_mock
1416:    if spawn_status == "mock_skip":
1417:        _release_lock(lock_path)
1418:        result_skip = {
1419:            **base_meta, "classification": "excluded_generated_or_runtime",
1420:            "verdict": None, "decision": "allow", "deny_reason": None,
1421:            "in_progress": False, "mock": "skip",
1422:        }
1423:        _auto_state_append({**result_skip, "event": "mock_skip"})
1424:        return result_skip
1425:
1426:    if spawn_status == "cmd_not_found":
1427:        _release_lock(lock_path)
1428:        result_cnf = {
1429:            **base_meta, "classification": "cmd_not_found",
1430:            "verdict": None, "decision": "deny",
1431:            "deny_reason": (
1432:                "codex CLI not found on PATH and CODEX_PLUGIN_ROOT not resolved. "
1433:                "Install codex CLI or set CODEX_PLUGIN_ROOT, then retry."
1434:            ),
1435:            "in_progress": False,
1436:        }
1437:        _auto_state_append({**result_cnf, "event": "cmd_not_found"})
1438:        return result_cnf
1439:
1440:    if spawn_status == "launch_error":
1441:        _release_lock(lock_path)
1442:        result_le = {
1443:            **base_meta, "classification": "sandbox_or_profile_config_error",
1444:            "verdict": None, "decision": "deny",
1445:            "deny_reason": (
1446:                f"codex spawn failed: {spawn_meta.get('error', '?')}. "
1447:                f"Check codex CLI install + CODEX_HOME profile."
1448:            ),
1449:            "in_progress": False,
1450:        }
1451:        _auto_state_append({**result_le, "event": "launch_error"})
1452:        return result_le
1453:
1454:    # spawn_status == "ok_started" → real codex Popen launched in background
1455:    spawn_pid = spawn_meta.get("pid", 0)
1456:    in_prog_spawned = {
1457:        **base_meta, "classification": "in_progress",
1458:        "verdict": None, "decision": "deny",
1459:        "deny_reason": (
1460:            f"AUTO-T1 IN PROGRESS for {target_path} "
1461:            f"(spawned codex PID {spawn_pid}, timeout {timeout_sec}s). "
1462:            f"Retry the SAME Edit in ~30s. Verdict will land at {out_path.name}."
1463:        ),
1464:        "in_progress": True,
1465:        "spawn_pid": spawn_pid,
1466:        "codex_argv": spawn_meta.get("codex_argv"),
1467:        "codex_home": spawn_meta.get("codex_home"),
1468:        "timeout_sec": timeout_sec,
1469:    }
1470:    _auto_state_append({**in_prog_spawned, "event": "spawned"})
1471:    return in_prog_spawned

1474:# --- existing pair-detection helpers (preserved verbatim) --------------
1475:
1476:
1477:def _consult_pairs(max_age_min: float) -> list[tuple[Path, Path, float]]:
1478:    """Yield (prompt_path, output_path, output_mtime) for valid pairs.
1479:
1480:    Per upstream codex T1 verdict section 1: a pair is valid iff the output exists,
1481:    is non-empty, has mtime >= companion-prompt mtime, and is within max_age_min
1482:    wall-time.
1483:    """
1484:    cutoff = _now_ts() - (max_age_min * 60.0)
1485:    pairs: list[tuple[Path, Path, float]] = []
1486:    if not _CONSULT_GLOB_DIR.is_dir():
1487:        return pairs
1488:    try:
1489:        candidates = []
1490:        for pattern in (  # non-authority literals: codex_consult output filename patterns, not cites
1491:            "codex_consult_*_OUT.txt",
1492:            "codex_consult_*_OUT.json",
1493:            "codex_consult_*_verdict.txt",
1494:            "codex_consult_*_verdict.json",
1495:        ):
1496:            candidates.extend(_CONSULT_GLOB_DIR.glob(pattern))
1497:    except OSError as exc:
1498:        log_swallow(exc, "codex_t1_gate.glob")
1499:        return pairs
1500:    for output_path in candidates:
1501:        try:
1502:            stem = output_path.stem
1503:            if stem.endswith("_OUT"):
1504:                prompt_stem = stem[: -len("_OUT")]
1505:            elif stem.endswith("_verdict"):
1506:                prompt_stem = stem[: -len("_verdict")]
1507:            else:
1508:                continue
1509:            for prompt_suffix in (".txt", ".json", ".md"):
1510:                prompt_path = output_path.with_name(prompt_stem + prompt_suffix)
1511:                if prompt_path.exists():
1512:                    break
1513:            else:
1514:                continue
1515:            out_stat = output_path.stat()
1516:            if out_stat.st_size == 0:
1517:                continue
1518:            if out_stat.st_mtime < cutoff:
1519:                continue
1520:            prompt_stat = prompt_path.stat()
1521:            if out_stat.st_mtime < prompt_stat.st_mtime:
1522:                continue
1523:            pairs.append((prompt_path, output_path, out_stat.st_mtime))
1524:        except OSError as exc:
1525:            log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.pair_stat:{output_path.name}")
1526:            continue
1527:    return pairs
1528:
1529:
1530:def _path_match_in_prompt(prompt_text: str, target_path: str) -> bool:
1531:    """Codex T1 prescription #5: exact-path or parent/basename match only.
1532:    Bare-basename match REMOVED — required parent dir evidence in prompt.
1533:    """
1534:    norm = _normalize(target_path)
1535:    candidates: set[str] = set()
1536:    if norm:
1537:        candidates.add(norm)
1538:        p = Path(norm)
1539:        parent = p.parent.name
1540:        base = p.name
1541:        if parent and base:
1542:            candidates.add(f"{parent}/{base}")
1543:    haystack = prompt_text  # prompt files preserve exact path strings
1544:    return any(c and c in haystack for c in candidates if len(c) >= 4)

1546:
1547:def _resolve_system_meta_review_pair(
1548:    target_path: str,
1549:    pairs: list[tuple[Path, Path, float]],
1550:    max_age_min: float,
1551:) -> tuple[bool, dict]:
1552:    """SYSTEM-meta-review fallback per .claude/rules/codex-t1-system-meta-review-fallback.md."""
1553:    _ = max_age_min
1554:    norm = _normalize(target_path)
1555:    if not norm:
1556:        return False, {}
1557:    base = Path(norm).name
1558:    parent = Path(norm).parent.name
1559:    candidates = [c for c in (norm, f"{parent}/{base}", base) if c and len(c) >= 4]
1560:    now = _now_ts()
1561:    for prompt_path, output_path, out_mtime in pairs:
1562:        _ = prompt_path
1563:        try:
1564:            output_text = output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:_OUTPUT_READ_BYTES]
1565:        except OSError as exc:
1566:            log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.meta_output_read:{output_path.name}")
1567:            continue
1568:        ot_lc = output_text.lower()
1569:        if not any(c.lower() in ot_lc for c in candidates):
1570:            continue
1571:        if '"verdict": "REJECT"' in output_text or "'verdict': 'REJECT'" in output_text:
1572:            continue
1573:        return True, {
1574:            "fallback": "system_meta_review",
1575:            "system_consult": str(output_path.name),
1576:            "age_min": round((now - out_mtime) / 60.0, 2),
1577:            "match_path": norm,
1578:        }
1579:    return False, {}

1581:
1582:def _detect_t1_present(target_path: str, max_age_min: float) -> tuple[bool, dict]:
1583:    """Return (t1_present, evidence_dict). Two-phase resolution preserved verbatim."""
1584:    pairs = _consult_pairs(max_age_min)
1585:    if not pairs:
1586:        return False, {"reason": "no_valid_pairs", "pairs_examined": 0}
1587:    pairs.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True)
1588:    now = _now_ts()
1589:    # User-explicit n=2 friction-class fix 2026-04-27 (post inspect_hook_dispatch.py audit):
1590:    # Add filename-stem fallback. The consult file IS named after the topic; matching
1591:    # the target file's basename stem against the prompt filename is a STRONGER evidence
1592:    # link than substring search through prompt body. Preserves codex revision #2
1593:    # exact-path-only precision via remaining body check; only adds permissive layer.
1594:    target_norm = _normalize(target_path)
1595:    target_stem = Path(target_norm).stem.lower() if target_norm else ""
1596:    for prompt_path, output_path, out_mtime in pairs:
1597:        try:
1598:            text = prompt_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:_PROMPT_READ_BYTES]
1599:        except OSError as exc:
1600:            log_swallow(exc, f"codex_t1_gate.prompt_read:{prompt_path.name}")
1601:            continue
1602:        prompt_name_lc = prompt_path.name.lower()
1603:        # Filename-stem match: target basename stem appears in consult filename. e.g.
1604:        # target="scripts/inspect_hook_dispatch.py" → stem="inspect_hook_dispatch" matches
1605:        # both "codex_consult_inspect_hook_dispatch.txt" and
1606:        # "codex_consult_inspect_hook_dispatch_design.txt" (non-authority
1607:        # literals — output filename examples, not cites).
1608:        # Length guard: stem must be ≥6 chars to avoid generic-name false-positives like "main".
1609:        filename_match = bool(target_stem) and len(target_stem) >= 6 and target_stem in prompt_name_lc
1610:        path_match = _path_match_in_prompt(text, target_path)
1611:        if filename_match or path_match:
1612:            return True, {
1613:                "prompt": str(prompt_path.name),
1614:                "output": str(output_path.name),
1615:                "age_min": round((now - out_mtime) / 60.0, 2),
1616:                "match_mode": (
1617:                    "filename_stem" if filename_match and not path_match
1618:                    else "path_in_prompt" if path_match and not filename_match
1619:                    else "both"
1620:                ),
1621:            }
1622:    found, evidence = _resolve_system_meta_review_pair(target_path, pairs, max_age_min)
1623:    if found:
1624:        return True, evidence
1625:    return False, {
1626:        "reason": "no_path_match",
1627:        "pairs_examined": len(pairs),
1628:        "newest_age_min": round((now - pairs[0][2]) / 60.0, 2) if pairs else None,
1629:        "system_meta_review_scanned": True,
1630:    }

1633:def _append_full_telemetry(row: dict) -> None:
1634:    """Append full per-record row to codex_t1_consult_gate.jsonl.
1635:
1636:    Ship #220 (codex T1 NEEDS-REVISION conf=0.91 F2): tags every NEW row with

109:def _emit_silent_fallback(status: str, detail: str, root: Path = _ROOT) -> None:
110:    """Sub-classify silent-fallback events + emit JSONL telemetry.
111:
112:    AXIS-3-002 P1 closure (iter-2 BUNDLE-2 Agent B 2026-04-27). Codex T1
113:    NEEDS-REVISION conf=0.88 verdict at .claude/state/
114:    codex_consult_lifecycle_e2e_audit_2026-04-27_OUT.txt:
115:        "T3/T4 detached review paths still contain silent skip/fallback
116:         modes. ... Sub-classify malformed_stdin, upstream_probe_failed,
117:         template_read_failed, and emit JSONL telemetry before any
118:         allow/skip decision."
119:
120:    Pattern source: Agent Y 7-class parse_failure sub-classifier in
121:    codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:_classify_parse_failure (commits e978550
122:    sss + b38a7a29 ccc; reference at
123:    Z:/claude/hooks/codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:136-164). This helper
124:    extends that family with silent_fallback_* sub-classes for the T3/T4
125:    detached-review code paths flagged in AXIS-3-002.
126:
127:    Stderr line is always emitted (fail-loud-via-stderr matches
128:    _append_telemetry pattern in codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:226-242).
129:    JSONL append is fail-open: telemetry write errors fall through
130:    silently rather than breaking the existing fail-open semantics on
131:    the host caller (the caller still returns its default {} / None /
132:    fallback-template; this helper is purely additive observability).
133:    """
134:    # SIM105: contextlib.suppress required for try-except-pass blocks per
135:    # ruff config; sibling hook codex_prepush_review.py uses identical pattern.
136:    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):  # noqa: BLE001 - stderr must never raise
137:        sys.stderr.write(f"codex_postcommit_review: {status} - {detail}\n")
138:    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):  # noqa: BLE001 - telemetry must not break fail-open
139:        state_dir = _state_dir_for(root)
140:        state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
141:        log_path = state_dir / "codex_postcommit_silent_fallback.jsonl"
142:        row = {
143:            "ts": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
144:            "status": status,
145:            "detail": detail,
146:        }
147:        with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
148:            f.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
149:
150:
151:# iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4 fix (cycle-after-clean-sota 2026-04-26):
152:# Wrong-SHA cross-project boundary fix.
153:#
154:# Root cause: hook ran `git rev-parse HEAD` with cwd=_ROOT (compile-time-bound
155:# to whichever project the hook script lives in). When CC is launched from
156:# Z:/claude/ (parent) but bash command runs `cd /z/claude-sota && git commit`,
157:# parent's hook would call git in parent's tree — captures the parent tree, NOT
158:# the just-committed claude-sota SHA. Evidence: parent JSONL recorded ALL T3
159:# launches today on parent SHA `9340c18f`, never on claude-sota commits.
160:#
161:# Fix is 2-part:
162:#  (A) extract just-committed SHA from tool_response.stdout via regex against
163:#      the `[<branch> <sha>] <msg>` git commit summary. Primary path; ground-
164:#      truth from the actual tool that ran the commit.
165:#  (B) detect actual repo root from tool_input.command `cd <path>` prefix
166:#      (highest fidelity — bash literally went there) > payload top-level cwd
167:#      > module _ROOT. Pass detected root as cwd= to ALL subprocess git calls.
168:#
169:# Source-cites:
170:#   - Anthropic hook payload spec at Z:/repos/deps/claude-code/plugins/plugin-dev/skills/hook-development/SKILL.md:300-316 @ 5bf19945e4e9e38d298ddc2befd5c30a7d504fb8 [content-SHA d1c0c199c70e8cef8fee8d3b792b0a27ff33046c]
171:#     confirms tool_response.stdout + top-level cwd field
172:#   - git print_summary at github.com/git/git/blob/master/builtin/commit.c
173:#     produces `[<branch> <sha>] <msg>` reliably (also `[<branch> (root-commit) <sha>]`,
174:#     `[detached HEAD <sha>]`)
175:_COMMIT_SHA_RE = re.compile(
176:    r"^\[(?:[^\s\]]+(?:\s+\(root-commit\))?|detached HEAD)\s+([0-9a-f]{7,40})\]",
177:    re.MULTILINE,
178:)
179:_CD_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(r"\bcd\s+([^\s&|;]+)")
180:
181:
182:def _resolve_target_root(cmd: str, payload_cwd: str) -> Path:
183:    """Find the repo root the commit actually ran in.
184:
185:    Priority: cd-prefix in command (highest fidelity — the bash actually went
186:    there) > payload top-level cwd > module _ROOT (compile-time fallback).
187:    Cycle-after-clean-sota 2026-04-26 iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4 fix.
188:    """
189:    for seg in re.split(r"\s*(?:&&|\|\||;)\s*", cmd.strip()):
190:        seg = seg.strip()
191:        m = _CD_PREFIX_RE.match(seg)
192:        if m:
193:            cand = m.group(1).strip("'\"")
194:            # Convert /z/claude-sota → Z:\claude-sota on Windows (Git Bash POSIX path)
195:            if cand.startswith("/") and len(cand) > 2 and cand[2] == "/":
196:                cand = f"{cand[1].upper()}:{cand[2:]}"
197:            p = Path(cand)
198:            if p.exists():
199:                # Walk up to find .git (handles bare git commit in monorepo subdir)
200:                for anc in [p, *p.parents]:
201:                    if (anc / ".git").exists():
202:                        return anc
203:                return p
204:    if payload_cwd:
205:        p = Path(payload_cwd)
206:        if p.exists() and (p / ".git").exists():
207:            return p
208:    return _ROOT

211:def _parse_stdin() -> dict:
212:    try:
213:        return json.load(sys.stdin)
214:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open on malformed stdin
215:        # AXIS-3-002 P1 (Agent B 2026-04-27): sub-classify silent fallback
216:        # + emit JSONL telemetry before fail-open default. See
217:        # _emit_silent_fallback docstring above for full rationale.
218:        _emit_silent_fallback("silent_fallback_malformed_stdin", repr(exc))
219:        return {}

222:def _is_commit_cmd(cmd: str) -> bool:
223:    """Match `git commit` including `git -C <path> commit` and `git -c key=val commit`.
224:
225:    Delegates to shared `_guard_base.git_verb_matches`. Agent A 2026-04-27 P1
226:    finding: prior `parts[i]=="git" and parts[i+1]=="commit"` rejected `-C/-c`
227:    forms because `parts[1]` was the flag, not the verb. n=6 git-dash-C class.
228:    """
229:    return git_verb_matches(cmd, "commit")

232:def _capture_diff_context(sha: str, root: Path = _ROOT) -> str:
233:    """Build a bounded diff context for the review prompt.
234:
235:    Cycle-438 hang-fix: Qwen3.6 context-derailment zone is 15-25K effective
236:    tokens per CLAUDE.md:66 (T1-confirmed on our hardware). Passing the diff
237:    via stdin pre-caps input instead of asking codex to run git+read files
238:    (which fans out to unbounded tool calls on monorepo commits).
239:
240:    Layers: stat → commit metadata → diff body (capped at 8000 chars to
241:    stay well below derailment zone even with Codex system overhead).
242:
243:    iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4: `root` parameter threads the actual repo root
244:    (resolved via _resolve_target_root) so cross-project commits review the
245:    correct repo, not parent CC's working scope.
246:    """
247:    parts: list[str] = []
248:    try:
249:        stat = subprocess.run(
250:            ["git", "show", sha, "--stat", "--format="],
251:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
252:        )
253:        if stat.returncode == 0 and stat.stdout:
254:            parts.append("=== git show --stat ===\n" + stat.stdout.strip())
255:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open on git errors + observability
256:        sys.stderr.write(f"[codex-postcommit] git show --stat failed: {exc!r}\n")
257:    try:
258:        meta = subprocess.run(
259:            ["git", "show", sha, "--format=fuller", "--no-patch"],
260:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
261:        )
262:        if meta.returncode == 0 and meta.stdout:
263:            parts.append("=== git show --format=fuller ===\n" + meta.stdout.strip())
264:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open + observability
265:        sys.stderr.write(f"[codex-postcommit] git show --format=fuller failed: {exc!r}\n")
266:    try:
267:        diff = subprocess.run(
268:            ["git", "show", sha, "--unified=3", "--format="],
269:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=False,
270:        )
271:        if diff.returncode == 0 and diff.stdout:
272:            diff_body = diff.stdout.strip()
273:            # Cap at 8000 chars (~2K tokens) well below derailment zone
274:            if len(diff_body) > 8000:
275:                diff_body = diff_body[:8000] + "\n...[diff truncated at 8000 chars]"
276:            parts.append("=== diff ===\n" + diff_body)
277:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open + observability
278:        sys.stderr.write(f"[codex-postcommit] git show --unified=3 failed: {exc!r}\n")
279:    return "\n\n".join(parts)

340:def _build_prompt(sha: str, diff_context: str) -> str:
341:    """Build review prompt from upstream adversarial-review.md template.
342:
343:    Reads the template file at invocation time so plugin bumps auto-propagate
344:    (no re-port needed per cycle). Interpolates the 4 placeholders, then
345:    prepends gstack boundary instruction (cycle-445 S1).
346:
347:    Fail-open: if template unreadable, fall back to minimal 4-block prompt
348:    preserving placeholder semantics.
349:    """
350:    try:
351:        template = _ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW_TEMPLATE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
352:    except OSError as exc:  # template missing or unreadable — fall back
353:        # AXIS-3-002 P1 (Agent B 2026-04-27): sub-classify silent fallback
354:        # + emit JSONL telemetry before fail-open template substitution. See
355:        # _emit_silent_fallback docstring above for full rationale.
356:        _emit_silent_fallback(
357:            "silent_fallback_template_read_failed",
358:            f"path={_ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW_TEMPLATE} error={exc!r}",
359:        )
360:        template = (
361:            "<task>Review {{TARGET_LABEL}}</task>\n"
362:            "<user_focus>{{USER_FOCUS}}</user_focus>\n"
363:            "<review_method>{{REVIEW_COLLECTION_GUIDANCE}}</review_method>\n"
364:            "<repository_context>{{REVIEW_INPUT}}</repository_context>\n"
365:        )
366:    populated = (
367:        template.replace("{{TARGET_LABEL}}", f"git commit {sha}")
368:        .replace(
369:            "{{USER_FOCUS}}",
370:            "General commit-scope review across all 7 attack-surface buckets.",
371:        )
372:        .replace(
373:            "{{REVIEW_COLLECTION_GUIDANCE}}",
374:            "The diff is pre-captured below. Do NOT run shell commands or "
375:            "read other files — work only from the provided context.",
376:        )
377:        .replace("{{REVIEW_INPUT}}", diff_context)
378:    )
379:    return f"{_GSTACK_BOUNDARY}\n\n{populated}"

382:def _launch_review(sha: str, root: Path = _ROOT) -> dict:
383:    # iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4: `root` parameter threads actual repo root from
384:    # _resolve_target_root() so cross-project commits review the correct repo.
385:    # Default _ROOT preserves backward compat for direct-launch from project dir.
386:    #
387:    # Cycle-451 preflight gate (gstack bin/gstack-codex-probe:19-50 + ECC
388:    # codex-companion.mjs setup --json convergent T1 sources). Fails fast on
389:    # missing-auth or CLI-not-on-PATH BEFORE spawning detached codex subprocess
390:    # to prevent cycle-444-class silent failures (service_tier=flex HTTP-400,
391:    # invalid auth, stdin-deadlock versions). known_bad_version is WARN-only
392:    # per gstack convention — proceed but surface to stderr.
393:    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
394:    from _codex_preflight import probe_codex  # noqa: E402
395:
396:    pf = probe_codex()
397:    sys.stderr.write(
398:        f"codex_postcommit_review: preflight cli_present={pf.cli_present} "
399:        f"version={pf.cli_version!r} auth_ok={pf.auth_ok} "
400:        f"auth_source={pf.auth_source} known_bad={pf.known_bad_version}\n",
401:    )
402:    for warning in pf.warnings:
403:        sys.stderr.write(f"codex_postcommit_review: WARN: {warning}\n")
404:    if not pf.ok:
405:        return {
406:            "sha": sha,
407:            "status": "skipped_preflight",
408:            "reason": "; ".join(pf.warnings) if pf.warnings else "preflight_not_ok",
409:            "cli_present": pf.cli_present,
410:            "auth_ok": pf.auth_ok,
411:            "ts": time.time(),
412:        }
413:
414:    # Cycle-479 BYPASS-SHIM: codex.CMD wraps `node codex.js` but on Windows
415:    # the cmd.exe shim breaks file-handle stdin/stdout inheritance under
416:    # DETACHED_PROCESS. Repro 2026-04-25:
417:    #   - codex.CMD + Popen(creationflags=0x208, stdout=file_fh) -> 0 bytes / TIMEOUT
418:    #   - node.exe + codex.js + same flags + same handles       -> 2957 bytes / 17.8s
419:    # Fixes 11/14 stuck_killed records (0 `completed` ever) on this machine.
420:    _cmd_path = shutil.which("codex")
421:    codex_argv0: list[str] = ["codex"]
422:    if _cmd_path:
423:        if _cmd_path.lower().endswith(".cmd"):
424:            _node = shutil.which("node.exe") or shutil.which("node")
425:            _js = Path(_cmd_path).parent / "node_modules" / "@openai" / "codex" / "bin" / "codex.js"
426:            if _node and _js.exists():
427:                codex_argv0 = [_node, str(_js)]
428:            else:
429:                # Cycle-481 (codex's own cycle-479 review verdict caught this gap):
430:                # refuse to fall back to .CMD shim — under DETACHED_PROCESS it produces
431:                # 0-byte streams. Skip with explicit reason so the missing review is
432:                # auditable rather than re-introducing the silent failure cycle-479 fixed.
433:                return {
434:                    "sha": sha,
435:                    "status": "skipped_no_direct_node",
436:                    "reason": f"codex.CMD shim at {_cmd_path} found but direct node resolution failed",
437:                    "node_found": _node is not None,
438:                    "codex_js_path": str(_js),
439:                    "codex_js_exists": _js.exists(),
440:                }
441:        else:
442:            codex_argv0 = [_cmd_path]
443:
444:    # Cycle-after-909 ship #1: data-boundary gate BEFORE diff-capture or prompt
445:    # construction. Per GPT-5.5 deep-review Finding 1 (P1 conf 0.94 D3) — the
446:    # documented 3-touchpoint workflow gate was advisory-only in
447:    # feedback_3touchpoint_codex_workflow.md; this call is what makes it
448:    # operational on the live T3 path. Fail-closed: if gate refuses, skip
449:    # codex spawn entirely and write status="gated" row.
450:    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
451:    from codex_gate import gate_review  # noqa: E402
452:
453:    # iter-50.10.fix5 (mirror of parent's iter-50.10.fix4 commit Z:/claude
454:    # d8de26a6 2026-04-26): pass cd-prefix-resolved git_root so gate's
455:    # `git diff-tree` runs in the actual repo (e.g. Z:/claude-sota when commit
456:    # happened there). Without this, gate's internal _resolve_git_root() may
457:    # return the wrong repo, causing git_diff_failed:<SHA> → gate refuses →
458:    # codex review SKIPPED on every cross-project commit.
459:    # Symmetric closure of the cd-prefix-chain pattern: iter-50.10.fix2 (commit
460:    # f151875) patched this file's local git invocations; iter-50.10.fix4 fixed
461:    # parent's gate-layer; this fix5 mirrors that gate-layer fix into claude-sota.
462:    gate_ok, gate_reason = gate_review("T3", rev_spec=sha, command_preview=f"codex_postcommit_review {sha[:8]}", git_root=root)
463:    if not gate_ok:
464:        # Iter-2 fix-forward (codex T3 on f6522446 HIGH conf=0.82): surface gated
465:        # decision to stderr so operators see it.
466:        # Cycle-after-909 ship #2 (Finding 3): ALSO enqueue to durable review queue
467:        # so gated commits aren't invisible debt. blocked_sensitive entries surface
468:        # at SessionStart and (next ship) block git push externalization.
469:        from codex_review_queue import enqueue as _queue_enqueue  # noqa: E402
470:
471:        # Try to read branch for queue subject; tolerate detached-HEAD failure
472:        # iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4: cwd=str(root) — was missing entirely (defaulted
473:        # to process cwd which leaks parent's repo on cross-project commits).
474:        branch = "unknown"
475:        try:
476:            br_result = subprocess.run(
477:                ["git", "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD"],
478:                cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
479:            )
480:            if br_result.returncode == 0:
481:                branch = br_result.stdout.strip() or "unknown"
482:        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
483:            pass
484:        # Iter-2 fix-forward (codex T3 on 69cea583 HIGH conf=0.92): do NOT
485:        # suppress enqueue failures. Catching explicitly and surfacing failure
486:        # closes the recursive silent-debt class — the queue is supposed to
487:        # END silent-skips, so failing to write to it must be loud, not quiet.
488:        queue_persisted = False
489:        queue_error: str | None = None
490:        try:
491:            entry_id = _queue_enqueue(
492:                touchpoint="T3-postcommit",
493:                subject={"head_sha": sha, "branch": branch},
494:                gate_status="gated",
495:                gate_reason=gate_reason or "unknown",
496:            )
497:            queue_persisted = entry_id is not None
498:        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-closed-with-loud-stderr per cycle-907
499:            queue_error = repr(exc)[:200]
500:
501:        sys.stderr.write(
502:            f"codex_postcommit_review: GATED — codex review SKIPPED for {sha[:8]}: {gate_reason}\n"
503:            f"  no codex audit ran on this commit. Trail at .claude/state/codex_postcommit_reviews.jsonl\n",
504:        )
505:        if queue_persisted:
506:            sys.stderr.write(
507:                "  enqueued at .claude/state/codex_review_queue.jsonl — see /codex-jsonl-status\n",
508:            )
509:        else:
510:            sys.stderr.write(
511:                f"  QUEUE PERSISTENCE FAILED ({queue_error or 'enqueue returned None — possibly disabled via env'}) "
512:                f"— this gated skip is NOT durable; manual triage required\n",
513:            )
514:        # Iter-4 fix-forward (codex T3 on 837709c4 HIGH conf=0.86): preserve
515:        # backwards-compatible `status="gated"` so existing consumers
516:        # (codex_invoke_rate, /codex-jsonl-status, codex_review_thread_bridge)
517:        # filtering on status==gated continue to count this row. Express the
518:        # durable-vs-failed distinction via ADDITIVE fields only:
519:        #   status_detail: "gated_durable" | "gated_no_queue"  (finer signal)
520:        #   queue_persisted: bool
521:        #   queue_error: str | None
522:        # New consumers opt into status_detail/queue_persisted; legacy
523:        # consumers see all gated rows uniformly (no signal loss either way).
524:        return {
525:            "sha": sha,
526:            "status": "gated",  # stable enum (cycle-after-909 iter-4)
527:            "status_detail": "gated_durable" if queue_persisted else "gated_no_queue",
528:            "reason": gate_reason,
529:            "queue_persisted": queue_persisted,
530:            "queue_error": queue_error if not queue_persisted else None,
531:            "ts": time.time(),
532:        }
533:
534:    sha8 = sha[:8] if sha else "unknown"
535:    # iter-50.55 Class 6 mirror: state_dir parametrized by target_root so
536:    # verdict files land at parent Z:/claude/.claude/state/ when commit
537:    # happened there (cd-prefix-chain rule extended to output-path resolution).
538:    state_dir = _state_dir_for(root)
539:    outfile = state_dir / f"codex_review_HEAD_{sha8}.txt"
540:    prompt_file = state_dir / f"codex_review_HEAD_{sha8}.prompt.txt"
541:    state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
542:
543:    diff_context = _capture_diff_context(sha, root=root)  # iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4
544:    instructions = _build_prompt(sha, diff_context)
545:
546:    # Bound input aggressively — cycle-438 derailment mitigation
547:    if len(instructions) > 12000:
548:        instructions = instructions[:12000] + "\n\n[prompt truncated]\n"
549:
550:    # Cycle-444: write prompt file for debug/audit visibility (version-controllable
551:    # in state/); still pass prompt content via argv since `codex exec` does NOT
552:    # support --prompt-file (only `codex-companion.mjs task` does per live-probe
553:    # 2026-04-24 `codex exec --prompt-file` → "unexpected argument '--prompt-file' found").
554:    # Windows CreateProcess argv cap is ~32K; our 12K-bounded prompt is safe.
555:    prompt_write_error: str | None = None
556:    try:
557:        prompt_file.write_text(instructions, encoding="utf-8")
558:    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open on prompt write (non-critical;
559:        # continue without file copy — argv still has the prompt)
560:        prompt_write_error = repr(e)[:200]
561:
562:    # Cycle-444 capability unleash (validated flags per `codex exec --help`):
563:    #   -p deep-review      — cycle-444 smoke-verified; Ollama providers hang with
564:    #                         --output-schema (gemma4:vision test 2026-04-24:
565:    #                         turn.started → timeout at 60s with schema; same
566:    #                         prompt without schema → 542 tokens clean in <60s).
567:    #                         GPT-5.5 via ChatGPT Pro auth handles schema
568:    #                         reliably; smoke 2026-04-24 returned perfect
569:    #                         {"verdict":"approve","summary":"...","findings":[],"next_steps":[]}
570:    #                         in 175 tokens. Pro is flat-rate subscription so
571:    #                         cost = quota-share, not dollars per cycle-435.
572:    #   -C <cwd>            — explicit cwd pin (ECC pattern orchestrate-codex-worker.sh:80)
573:    #   --color never       — ECC pattern (same), strip ANSI for clean output parsing
574:    #   --output-schema     — cycle-435 surfaced + cycle-444 first production use;
575:    #                         strict JSON per schemas/review-output.schema.json
576:    #                         (staged in state/ for stable path across plugin updates)
577:    #   --output-last-message — final agent_message to file
578:    #   --json              — structured event stream on stdout
579:    #   --ephemeral         — DEFAULT (env-gated since cycle-590).
580:    #                         No thread persistence; we re-review per commit.

651:    # findings (codex_review_HEAD_aa09708b.txt conf=0.77 + 0.74 medium) — codex
652:    # correctly identified two residual gaps: (1) truncated UUID hex[:12] is
653:    # 48-bit which has finite birthday-risk (~10^-7 over 10K launches), and
654:    # (2) "wb" truncate-write would silently overwrite if collision occurs.
655:    # Both gaps eliminated here:
656:    #   - FULL hex (32 chars = 128 bits) → birthday-risk ~10^-19 per pair
657:    #     (effectively zero, beyond physical observability)
658:    #   - O_CREAT|O_EXCL → fs-syscall-guaranteed uniqueness via FileExistsError
659:    #     on collision, retry with fresh UUID; defense-in-depth even if the
660:    #     128-bit UUID somehow collides (it cannot at our scale)
661:    # Closed-loop arc: ship 16 (collision-guaranteed for repeats, codex high)
662:    # → ship 17 (probabilistic ts, codex high) → ship 18 (cryptographic
663:    # truncated UUID, codex medium) → ship 19 (atomic full UUID, target
664:    # codex low or approve). Per rules/closed-loop-recursive-narrowing.md
665:    # cycle-539 monotone-decline pattern.
666:    stream_file = None
667:    stream_fh = None
668:    for _ in range(8):  # bounded retries — collision is unobservable, retries are belt+suspenders
669:        # iter-50.55 Class 6 mirror: state_dir already parametrized by target_root above
670:        candidate = state_dir / f"codex_review_HEAD_{sha8}_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.stream.jsonl"
671:        try:
672:            # Atomic exclusive-create: fails with FileExistsError if path exists.
673:            # O_BINARY is no-op on POSIX (getattr default 0); ensures no text-mode
674:            # translation on Windows.
675:            _binary_flag = getattr(os, "O_BINARY", 0)
676:            _fd = os.open(candidate, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY | _binary_flag, 0o644)
677:            stream_fh = os.fdopen(_fd, "wb")  # noqa: SIM115 — fh lives past Popen
678:            stream_file = candidate
679:            break
680:        except FileExistsError:
681:            continue  # retry with fresh UUID — astronomically unlikely
682:        except OSError as e:
683:            return {"sha": sha, "status": "stream_open_failed", "error": repr(e)[:200]}
684:    if stream_fh is None or stream_file is None:
685:        # 8 consecutive 128-bit UUID collisions = ~10^-152 — should never happen.
686:        # Fail loudly per codex review recommendation.
687:        return {"sha": sha, "status": "stream_path_exhausted", "error": "8 atomic-create retries failed"}

690:    # only applies to PIPES; regular files are buffer-free from parent's view).
691:    try:

693:    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
694:        stream_fh.close()
695:        return {"sha": sha, "status": "prompt_stdin_open_failed", "error": repr(e)[:200]}

697:    popen_kwargs: dict = {
698:        "stdin": prompt_stdin_fh,
699:        "stdout": stream_fh,
700:        "stderr": stream_fh,
701:        "close_fds": True,
702:        # iter-45 fix-forward: explicit CODEX_HOME prevents codex CLI from
703:        # reading parent's ~/.codex/config.toml when claude-sota's hook is
704:        # invoked under ccc-mediated mode (HOME=Z:/claude/). Without this,
705:        # codex looks for `deep-review-exec` profile in parent's config
706:        # which doesn't define it, fails with `Error: config profile
707:        # 'deep-review-exec' not found`. Pattern B sub-cause 3 per
708:        # rules/codex-t1-fix-forward-pattern.md (n=2 codification).
709:        # Honor ambient CODEX_HOME (set by tools/eee.ps1 to state-outside-repo
710:        # Z:/claude-sota-installed-state/.codex per CLAUDE.local.md (e) discipline)
711:        # when present; fall back to repo-root .codex otherwise. Wave 50 fire 35 P1-2
712:        # fix-forward per Agent JJ adversarial at tmp/wave50-fire35-agentJJ-installed-
713:        # runtime-adversarial-2026-05-06.md.
714:        "env": {**os.environ, "CODEX_HOME": os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME", str(_ROOT / ".codex"))},
715:    }
716:    if sys.platform.startswith("win"):
717:        # 0x00000008 DETACHED_PROCESS: child does NOT inherit parent console
718:        # 0x00000200 CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP: child gets own group (Ctrl-C isolation)
719:        # 0x08000000 CREATE_NO_WINDOW: child does NOT allocate a new console window
720:        # Cycle-590 add CREATE_NO_WINDOW: DETACHED_PROCESS alone removes parent
721:        # console inheritance but does NOT prevent the child (codex.exe, Rust
722:        # console app) from allocating its own — Windows would flash a brief
723:        # console window per spawn, visible to the user during /loop fires +
724:        # any commit. With stdin/stdout/stderr already redirected to file
725:        # handles via stream_fh + prompt_stdin_fh (cycle-444 stdin-pivot),
726:        # CREATE_NO_WINDOW is safe: codex's println!/eprintln! macros write
727:        # to the redirected fds regardless of console presence.
728:        popen_kwargs["creationflags"] = 0x00000008 | 0x00000200 | 0x08000000
729:        # SOTA windowsHide-equivalent (2026-04-26): grounded in openai/codex-plugin-cc
730:        # TIER-1 source Z:/repos/deps/codex-plugin-cc/plugins/codex/scripts/lib/app-server.mjs:194 @ 807e03ac9d5aa23bc395fdec8c3767500a86b3cf [content-SHA 127c837612fbd65dcf33238f97c5584a13390da8]
731:        # plus process.mjs:13 @ same SHA [content-SHA af28d1cf5b4844c59365f209fd7070f7e5a57afe] — both pass `windowsHide: true` to node's
732:        # spawn(). The Python equivalent is STARTUPINFO with STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW +
733:        # SW_HIDE. Belt-and-suspenders with CREATE_NO_WINDOW above: STARTUPINFO+SW_HIDE
734:        # explicitly hides any window the rust grandchild tries to allocate.
735:        startupinfo = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
736:        startupinfo.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
737:        startupinfo.wShowWindow = subprocess.SW_HIDE
738:        popen_kwargs["startupinfo"] = startupinfo
739:    else:
740:        popen_kwargs["start_new_session"] = True

742:    try:
743:        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, **popen_kwargs)
744:        record: dict = {
745:            "sha": sha,
746:            "pid": proc.pid,
747:            "outfile": str(outfile),
748:            "prompt_file": str(prompt_file),

755:        }

762:        return {"sha": sha, "status": "launch_error", "error": repr(e)[:200]}

765:def _append_jsonl(row: dict, root: Path = _ROOT) -> None:
766:    """Append one JSONL row to codex_postcommit_reviews.jsonl under target root.
767:
768:    iter-50.55 Class 6 mirror: `root` defaults to `_ROOT` for back-compat with
769:    any callers that haven't been threaded yet, but main()/launch flow passes
770:    the `_target_root` resolved by `_resolve_target_root()` so cross-project
771:    commits write their evidence trail to the actual repo's state dir, not
772:    claude-sota's frozen-at-import dir.
773:    """
774:    row["ts"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())
775:    state_dir = _state_dir_for(root)
776:    launch_log = state_dir / "codex_postcommit_reviews.jsonl"
777:    try:
778:        state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
779:        with launch_log.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
780:            f.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
781:    except Exception as e:
782:        # Fail-open — log-write failures must not block the commit
783:        print(f"codex_postcommit_review: jsonl write failed: {e!r}", file=sys.stderr)

786:def main() -> int:
787:    if os.environ.get("CODEX_POSTCOMMIT_REVIEW_DISABLE") == "1":
788:        return 0
789:
790:    # iter-25.3.1 §4: capture main-entry monotonic for commit_duration_ms on
791:    # _launch_review() return rows (main-entry-to-row latency).
792:    _main_start_ms = time.monotonic()
793:
794:    payload = _parse_stdin()
795:
796:    # Belt-and-suspenders: settings.json if-filter is primary, but re-check here
797:    tool_name = payload.get("tool_name") or payload.get("toolName") or ""
798:    tool_input = payload.get("tool_input", {}) or {}
799:    cmd_str = tool_input.get("command", "")
800:    tool_response = payload.get("tool_response", {}) or {}
801:    exit_code = tool_response.get("exitCode")
802:
803:    # iter-50.55 Class 6 mirror: resolve _target_root EARLY (before main_entered
804:    # instrumentation) so ALL JSONL rows from this hook invocation land at the
805:    # actual repo's state dir. Without this, the main_entered row would go to
806:    # claude-sota's state and the launched/gated/etc rows go to target's state
807:    # — split evidence trail across two repos defeats Class 6's purpose.
808:    payload_cwd = payload.get("cwd", "") or ""
809:    _target_root = _resolve_target_root(cmd_str, payload_cwd) if cmd_str else _ROOT
810:
811:    # Cycle-485 INSTRUMENTATION (cycle-461 git-diff-verify dogfood): write
812:    # "main_entered" marker BEFORE filters so JSONL audit distinguishes
813:    # "hook not dispatched" (no marker row) from "hook dispatched but
814:    # filter-rejected" (marker row + no launched). Iter-20 caught that
815:    # cycle-479 LIVE-VERIFIED claim was strictly manual-stdin-invoke; auto
816:    # path silently rejected somewhere — this surfaces which filter.
817:    _append_jsonl({
818:        "status": "main_entered",
819:        "duration_ms": int((time.monotonic() - _HOOK_IMPORT_MS) * 1000),
820:        "tool_name": tool_name,
821:        "cmd_preview": cmd_str[:200],
822:        "exit_code": exit_code,
823:    }, root=_target_root)
824:
825:    if tool_name != "Bash":
826:        return 0
827:    if not _is_commit_cmd(cmd_str):
828:        return 0
829:
830:    # Skip failed commits (no new SHA to review)
831:    if exit_code not in (0, None):
832:        return 0
833:
834:    # iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4 fix: _target_root resolution moved up (iter-50.55
835:    # Class 6 mirror) so main_entered and launched rows land at same target dir.
836:    # Was: git rev-parse HEAD with cwd=_ROOT (compile-time-bound to parents[3] =
837:    # Z:/claude-sota). Without this, cross-project commits (CC launched from
838:    # claude-sota but `cd /z/claude && git commit`) captured claude-sota's HEAD
839:    # instead of the just-committed parent SHA.
840:
841:    # iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4: extract just-committed SHA from tool_response.stdout
842:    # via _COMMIT_SHA_RE regex (primary, ground-truth from the actual git that ran).
843:    # Fallback to git rev-parse HEAD with explicit cwd=_target_root if regex misses
844:    # (e.g. --quiet flag suppressing stdout). Resolve abbreviated SHA to full 40-char
845:    # via target repo for cleanliness.
846:    sha = ""
847:    stdout_text = tool_response.get("stdout", "") or ""
848:    m = _COMMIT_SHA_RE.search(stdout_text)
849:    if m:
850:        short = m.group(1)
851:        try:
852:            full = subprocess.run(
853:                ["git", "rev-parse", short],
854:                cwd=str(_target_root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
855:            )
856:            full_stripped = full.stdout.strip()
857:            # Abbreviated SHA acceptable as fallback; downstream sha8 truncation safe.
858:            sha = full_stripped if (full.returncode == 0 and full_stripped) else short
859:        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open per cycle-279
860:            sha = short
861:    if not sha:
862:        # Fallback: git rev-parse HEAD with EXPLICIT cwd=_target_root (was cwd=_ROOT bug)
863:        try:
864:            sha_result = subprocess.run(
865:                ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
866:                cwd=str(_target_root),
867:                capture_output=True,
868:                text=True,
869:                timeout=5,
870:                check=False,
871:            )
872:            sha = sha_result.stdout.strip()
873:            if not sha or sha_result.returncode != 0:
874:                return 0  # No HEAD or git failed — fail-open
875:        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
876:            print(f"codex_postcommit_review: rev-parse failed: {e!r}", file=sys.stderr)
877:            return 0
878:
879:    row = _launch_review(sha, root=_target_root)  # iter-50.10.fix2 Class 4
880:    # iter-25.3.1 §4: enrich row with commit_duration_ms (main-entry-to-row latency)
881:    # before append. Centralized per codex T1 NEEDS-REVISION 0.88 Option C —
882:    # avoids touching every _launch_review return dict.
883:    row["commit_duration_ms"] = int((time.monotonic() - _main_start_ms) * 1000)
884:    _append_jsonl(row, root=_target_root)  # iter-50.55 Class 6 mirror: thread root for output-path
885:    return 0

889:    sys.exit(main())

102:def _windowless_kwargs() -> dict:
103:    if not _WIN32:
104:        return {}
105:    si = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
106:    si.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
107:    si.wShowWindow = subprocess.SW_HIDE
108:    return {"creationflags": subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW, "startupinfo": si}

152:def _emit_silent_fallback(status: str, detail: str, root: Path = _ROOT) -> None:
153:    """Sub-classify silent-fallback events + emit JSONL telemetry.
154:
155:    AXIS-3-002 P1 closure (iter-2 BUNDLE-2 Agent B 2026-04-27). Codex T1
156:    NEEDS-REVISION conf=0.88 verdict at .claude/state/
157:    codex_consult_lifecycle_e2e_audit_2026-04-27_OUT.txt:
158:        "T3/T4 detached review paths still contain silent skip/fallback
159:         modes. ... Sub-classify malformed_stdin, upstream_probe_failed,
160:         template_read_failed, and emit JSONL telemetry before any
161:         allow/skip decision."
162:
163:    Pattern source: Agent Y 7-class parse_failure sub-classifier in
164:    codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:_classify_parse_failure (commits e978550
165:    sss + b38a7a29 ccc; reference at
166:    Z:/claude/hooks/codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:136-164). This helper
167:    extends that family with silent_fallback_* sub-classes for the T3/T4
168:    detached-review code paths flagged in AXIS-3-002.
169:
170:    Stderr line is always emitted (fail-loud-via-stderr matches
171:    _append_telemetry pattern in codex_t2_pre_commit_gate.py:226-242).
172:    JSONL append is fail-open: telemetry write errors fall through
173:    silently rather than breaking the existing fail-open semantics on
174:    the host caller (the caller still returns its default {} / None /
175:    fallback-template; this helper is purely additive observability).
176:    """
177:    # SIM105: contextlib.suppress required for try-except-pass blocks per
178:    # ruff config; matches port-note discipline rules/audit-action-loop.md.
179:    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):  # noqa: BLE001 - stderr must never raise
180:        sys.stderr.write(f"codex_prepush_review: {status} - {detail}\n")
181:    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):  # noqa: BLE001 - telemetry must not break fail-open
182:        state_dir = _state_dir_for(root)
183:        state_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
184:        log_path = state_dir / "codex_prepush_silent_fallback.jsonl"
185:        row = {
186:            "ts": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
187:            "status": status,
188:            "detail": detail,
189:        }
190:        with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
191:            f.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

239:def _is_push_cmd(cmd: str) -> bool:
240:    """Match `git push` including `git -C <path> push` and `git -c key=val push`.
241:
242:    Delegates to shared `_guard_base.git_verb_matches` which excludes embedded
243:    references (`git log push:bar`) and walks past flag tokens before matching
244:    the verb. Agent A 2026-04-27 P1 finding closes the same n=6 git-dash-C class
245:    on the prepush surface.
246:    """
247:    return git_verb_matches(cmd, "push")

251:# parent's iter-50.10.fix6 (commit Z:/claude 04617d95). Closes T4 prepush
252:# cd-prefix-chain in claude-sota's mirror hook. n=3 of cd-prefix-chain pattern
253:# (per cycle-322 jurisdiction triggers automatic rule-layer promotion).
254:_CD_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(r"\bcd\s+([^\s&|;]+)")

257:def _resolve_target_root(cmd: str, payload_cwd: str) -> Path:
258:    """Find the repo root the push actually ran in.
259:
260:    Priority: cd-prefix in command (highest fidelity — bash literally went
261:    there) > payload top-level cwd > module _ROOT (compile-time fallback).
262:    iter-50.10.fix7 (mirror of parent's iter-50.10.fix6 _resolve_target_root).
263:    """
264:    for seg in re.split(r"\s*(?:&&|\|\||;)\s*", cmd.strip()):
265:        seg = seg.strip()
266:        m = _CD_PREFIX_RE.match(seg)
267:        if m:
268:            cand = m.group(1).strip("'\"")
269:            # Convert /z/claude-sota → Z:\claude-sota on Windows
270:            if cand.startswith("/") and len(cand) > 2 and cand[2] == "/":
271:                cand = f"{cand[1].upper()}:{cand[2:]}"
272:            p = Path(cand)
273:            if p.exists():
274:                # Walk up to find .git
275:                for anc in [p, *p.parents]:
276:                    if (anc / ".git").exists():
277:                        return anc
278:                return p
279:    if payload_cwd:
280:        p = Path(payload_cwd)
281:        if p.exists() and (p / ".git").exists():
282:            return p
283:    return _ROOT

285:
286:def _detect_base_ref(root: Path = _ROOT) -> str | None:
287:    """Detect upstream base ref to diff against. 4-tier fallback.
288:
289:    Order:
290:      1. Current branch's tracking remote ref (`@{upstream}`) — most accurate
291:      2. origin/HEAD via `git symbolic-ref` — repo-wide default
292:      3. origin/main if present
293:      4. origin/master if present (legacy default name)
294:
295:    Returns None if no remote/upstream — caller skips review with
296:    status: "skipped_no_upstream".
297:
298:    iter-50.10.fix7: accept `root` param for cross-project cd-prefix support.
299:    Default _ROOT preserves backward compat for legacy callers.
300:    """
301:    # 1. Tracking branch
302:    try:
303:        result = subprocess.run(
304:            ["git", "rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "@{upstream}"],
305:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
306:            **_windowless_kwargs(),
307:        )
308:        if result.returncode == 0 and result.stdout.strip():
309:            return result.stdout.strip()
310:    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open through fallback chain
311:        pass
312:    # 2. origin/HEAD
313:    try:
314:        result = subprocess.run(
315:            ["git", "symbolic-ref", "refs/remotes/origin/HEAD"],
316:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
317:            **_windowless_kwargs(),
318:        )
319:        if result.returncode == 0 and result.stdout.strip():
320:            return result.stdout.strip().replace("refs/remotes/", "", 1)
321:    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
322:        pass
323:    # 3-4. origin/main, origin/master
324:    for candidate in ("origin/main", "origin/master"):
325:        try:
326:            result = subprocess.run(
327:                ["git", "rev-parse", "--verify", candidate],
328:                cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3, check=False,
329:                **_windowless_kwargs(),
330:            )
331:            if result.returncode == 0:
332:                return candidate
333:        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
334:            pass
335:    # AXIS-3-002 P1 (Agent B 2026-04-27): all 4 fallback tiers exhausted →
336:    # emit silent_fallback_upstream_probe_failed telemetry before fail-open
337:    # `return None`. Codex T1 verdict at codex_consult_lifecycle_e2e_audit_
338:    # 2026-04-27_OUT.txt explicitly flags this terminal None as the silent
339:    # skip mode that callers ignore. See _emit_silent_fallback above.
340:    _emit_silent_fallback(
341:        "silent_fallback_upstream_probe_failed",
342:        "all 4 tiers (tracking-branch, origin/HEAD, origin/main, origin/master) returned no upstream",
343:        root=root,
344:    )
345:    return None

347:
348:def _capture_diff_context(base: str, root: Path = _ROOT) -> str:
349:    """Build a bounded cumulative-branch diff context for the review prompt.
350:
351:    Diffs `base..HEAD` (cumulative) instead of cycle-437's `HEAD~1..HEAD`
352:    (per-commit). Same 8000-char cap to stay below Qwen3.6 derailment zone
353:    per CLAUDE.md:66 (15-25K effective tokens). Layers: stat → log → diff body.
354:    """
355:    parts: list[str] = []
356:    range_spec = f"{base}..HEAD"
357:    try:
358:        stat = subprocess.run(
359:            ["git", "diff", "--stat", range_spec],
360:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
361:            **_windowless_kwargs(),
362:        )
363:        if stat.returncode == 0 and stat.stdout:
364:            parts.append(f"=== git diff --stat {range_spec} ===\n" + stat.stdout.strip())
365:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open + observability
366:        sys.stderr.write(f"[codex-prepush] git diff --stat failed: {exc!r}\n")
367:    try:
368:        log = subprocess.run(
369:            ["git", "log", range_spec, "--format=%h %s"],
370:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,
371:            **_windowless_kwargs(),
372:        )
373:        if log.returncode == 0 and log.stdout:
374:            parts.append(f"=== git log {range_spec} ===\n" + log.stdout.strip())
375:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open + observability
376:        sys.stderr.write(f"[codex-prepush] git log failed: {exc!r}\n")
377:    try:
378:        diff = subprocess.run(
379:            ["git", "diff", "--unified=3", range_spec],
380:            cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=False,
381:            **_windowless_kwargs(),
382:        )
383:        if diff.returncode == 0 and diff.stdout:
384:            diff_body = diff.stdout.strip()
385:            if len(diff_body) > 8000:
386:                diff_body = diff_body[:8000] + "\n...[diff truncated at 8000 chars]"
387:            parts.append("=== diff ===\n" + diff_body)
388:    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-open + observability
389:        sys.stderr.write(f"[codex-prepush] git diff --unified=3 failed: {exc!r}\n")
390:    return "\n\n".join(parts)

393:def _build_prompt(branch: str, base: str, diff_context: str) -> str:
394:    """Build pre-push review prompt from upstream adversarial-review.md template.
395:
396:    Differs from cycle-437 sibling only in TARGET_LABEL + USER_FOCUS framing —
397:    the prompt explicitly notes the cumulative scope so the reviewer scrutinizes
398:    inter-commit consistency rather than re-reviewing per-commit findings.
399:    """
400:    try:
401:        template = _ADVERSARIAL_REVIEW_TEMPLATE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
402:    except OSError as exc:  # template missing — fall back to minimal 4-block + observability
403:        sys.stderr.write(f"[codex-prepush] template read failed: {exc!r}; using minimal 4-block fallback\n")
404:        template = (
405:            "<task>Review {{TARGET_LABEL}}</task>\n"
406:            "<user_focus>{{USER_FOCUS}}</user_focus>\n"
407:            "<review_method>{{REVIEW_COLLECTION_GUIDANCE}}</review_method>\n"
408:            "<repository_context>{{REVIEW_INPUT}}</repository_context>\n"
409:        )
410:    populated = (
411:        template.replace(
412:            "{{TARGET_LABEL}}",
413:            f"branch {branch} pre-push (cumulative {base}..HEAD)",
414:        )
415:        .replace(
416:            "{{USER_FOCUS}}",
417:            "Cumulative-branch review: scrutinize inter-commit consistency, "
418:            "incomplete refactors split across commits, and helpers introduced "
419:            "in early commits but mis-used in later ones. Per-commit findings "
420:            "are already covered by the post-commit reviewer; focus here on "
421:            "what is only visible at branch scope.",
422:        )
423:        .replace(
424:            "{{REVIEW_COLLECTION_GUIDANCE}}",
425:            "The cumulative diff is pre-captured below. Do NOT run shell "
426:            "commands or read other files — work only from the provided context.",
427:        )
428:        .replace("{{REVIEW_INPUT}}", diff_context)
429:    )
430:    return f"{_GSTACK_BOUNDARY}\n\n{populated}"

433:def _launch_review(branch: str, base: str, sha: str, root: Path = _ROOT) -> dict:
434:    # Cycle-451 preflight gate — same gstack+ECC convergent T1 as cycle-437.
435:    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
436:    from _codex_preflight import probe_codex  # noqa: E402
437:
438:    pf = probe_codex()
439:    sys.stderr.write(
440:        f"codex_prepush_review: preflight cli_present={pf.cli_present} "
441:        f"version={pf.cli_version!r} auth_ok={pf.auth_ok} "
442:        f"auth_source={pf.auth_source} known_bad={pf.known_bad_version}\n",
443:    )
444:    for warning in pf.warnings:
445:        sys.stderr.write(f"codex_prepush_review: WARN: {warning}\n")
446:    if not pf.ok:
447:        return {
448:            "branch": branch,
449:            "base": base,
450:            "sha": sha,
451:            "status": "skipped_preflight",
452:            "reason": "; ".join(pf.warnings) if pf.warnings else "preflight_not_ok",
453:            "cli_present": pf.cli_present,
454:            "auth_ok": pf.auth_ok,
455:        }
456:
457:    # Cycle-479 BYPASS-SHIM (mirrors codex_postcommit_review.py:217): codex.CMD
458:    # wraps `node codex.js` but on Windows the cmd.exe shim breaks file-handle
459:    # stdin/stdout inheritance under DETACHED_PROCESS. Direct node invocation
460:    # works (probe 2026-04-25: shim 0 bytes / TIMEOUT vs node 2957 bytes / 17.8s).
461:    _cmd_path = shutil.which("codex")
462:    codex_argv0: list[str] = ["codex"]
463:    if _cmd_path:
464:        if _cmd_path.lower().endswith(".cmd"):
465:            _node = shutil.which("node.exe") or shutil.which("node")
466:            _js = Path(_cmd_path).parent / "node_modules" / "@openai" / "codex" / "bin" / "codex.js"
467:            if _node and _js.exists():
468:                codex_argv0 = [_node, str(_js)]
469:            else:
470:                # Cycle-481 (codex's own cycle-479 review verdict caught this gap):
471:                # refuse to fall back to .CMD shim — under DETACHED_PROCESS it produces
472:                # 0-byte streams. Skip with explicit reason so the missing review is
473:                # auditable rather than re-introducing the silent failure cycle-479 fixed.
474:                return {
475:                    "branch": branch,
476:                    "base": base,
477:                    "sha": sha,
478:                    "status": "skipped_no_direct_node",
479:                    "reason": f"codex.CMD shim at {_cmd_path} found but direct node resolution failed",
480:                    "node_found": _node is not None,
481:                    "codex_js_path": str(_js),
482:                    "codex_js_exists": _js.exists(),
483:                }
484:        else:
485:            codex_argv0 = [_cmd_path]
486:
487:    # Cycle-after-909 ship #1: data-boundary gate BEFORE diff-capture or prompt
488:    # construction. Per GPT-5.5 deep-review Finding 1 (P1 conf 0.94 D3) — same
489:    # call as codex_postcommit_review.py but with rev_spec=base..HEAD range
490:    # (T1 consult correction #1: T3 accepts rev_spec, NOT sha-shaped). Fail-
491:    # closed: gate refusal skips codex spawn and writes status="gated".
492:    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
493:    from codex_gate import gate_review  # noqa: E402
494:
495:    range_spec = f"{base}..HEAD"
496:    # iter-50.10.fix7: pass cd-prefix-resolved git_root so gate's git diff-tree
497:    # runs in the actual repo. Mirror of parent's iter-50.10.fix6 fix.
498:    gate_ok, gate_reason = gate_review("T3", rev_spec=range_spec, command_preview=f"codex_prepush_review {range_spec}", git_root=root)
499:    if not gate_ok:
500:        # Iter-2 fix-forward (codex T3 on f6522446 HIGH conf=0.82): surface gated
501:        # decision to stderr; mirrors codex_postcommit_review.py iter-2.
502:        # Cycle-after-909 ship #2 (Finding 3): ALSO enqueue to durable review
503:        # queue with IMMUTABLE subject (resolved oids, not range_spec which
504:        # mutates as origin/main advances — per T1 consult correction #2).
505:        from codex_review_queue import enqueue as _queue_enqueue  # noqa: E402
506:
507:        # Resolve immutable oids for the queue subject
508:        base_oid = base
509:        head_oid = sha
510:        with contextlib.suppress(OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
511:            br = subprocess.run(
512:                ["git", "rev-parse", base],
513:                cwd=str(root), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=False,  # iter-50.10.fix7
514:                **_windowless_kwargs(),
515:            )
516:            if br.returncode == 0:
517:                base_oid = br.stdout.strip() or base
518:        # Iter-2 fix-forward (codex T3 on 69cea583 HIGH conf=0.92): mirrors
519:        # codex_postcommit_review.py iter-2 — do NOT suppress enqueue failures.
520:        # The queue is supposed to END silent-skips, so failing to write to it
521:        # must be loud, not quiet (closes recursive silent-debt class).
522:        queue_persisted = False
523:        queue_error: str | None = None
524:        try:
525:            entry_id = _queue_enqueue(
526:                touchpoint="T3-prepush",
527:                subject={
528:                    "base_oid": base_oid,
529:                    "head_oid": head_oid,
530:                    "branch": branch,
531:                    "base_ref": base,
532:                },
533:                gate_status="gated",
534:                gate_reason=gate_reason or "unknown",
535:            )
536:            queue_persisted = entry_id is not None
537:        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — fail-closed-with-loud-stderr per cycle-907
538:            queue_error = repr(exc)[:200]
539:
540:        sys.stderr.write(
541:            f"codex_prepush_review: GATED — codex review SKIPPED for {branch} ({range_spec}): {gate_reason}\n"
542:            f"  no codex audit ran on this push. Trail at .claude/state/codex_prepush_reviews.jsonl\n",
543:        )

745:def _append_jsonl(row: dict, root: Path = _ROOT) -> None:
746:    """Append one JSONL row to codex_prepush_reviews.jsonl under target root.

753:    """
754:    row["ts"] = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())

756:    launch_log = state_dir / "codex_prepush_reviews.jsonl"

761:    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
762:        print(f"codex_prepush_review: jsonl write failed: {e!r}", file=sys.stderr)

44:def win32(path):

48:def discover_plugin_roots():
49:    roots = []
50:    for runtime in RUNTIMES:
51:        runtime_root = pathlib.Path(runtime)
52:        cache_root = runtime_root / ".claude" / "plugins" / "cache"
53:        for org, name in ECC_CACHE_LAYOUTS:
54:            base = cache_root / org / name
55:            if base.is_dir():
56:                for version_dir in sorted(base.iterdir()):
57:                    if version_dir.is_dir():
58:                        roots.append(version_dir)
59:
60:        marketplace_root = runtime_root / ".claude" / "plugins" / "marketplaces"
61:        for name in ECC_MARKETPLACE_NAMES:
62:            candidate = marketplace_root / name
63:            if candidate.is_dir():
64:                roots.append(candidate)
65:
66:    deduped = []
67:    seen = set()
68:    for root in roots:
69:        key = str(root.resolve()).lower()
70:        if key not in seen:
71:            seen.add(key)
72:            deduped.append(root)
73:    return deduped

76:def discover_hooks_json_files():
77:    files = []
78:    for root in discover_plugin_roots():

80:            candidate = root.joinpath(*rel)

86:def iter_command_refs(node):
87:    if isinstance(node, dict):
88:        if isinstance(node.get("command"), str):
89:            yield node
90:        for value in node.values():
91:            yield from iter_command_refs(value)
92:    elif isinstance(node, list):
93:        for item in node:
94:            yield from iter_command_refs(item)

97:def derive_plugin_root(hooks_json_path):
98:    parent = hooks_json_path.parent
99:    if parent.name in ("hooks", ".cursor"):
100:        return parent.parent
101:    return parent
