104:def extract_inline_tail(command):
105:    if INLINE_BOOTSTRAP_MARKER not in command:
106:        return None
107:    for marker in INLINE_BOOTSTRAP_TAIL_MARKERS:
108:        idx = command.find(marker)
109:        if idx != -1:
110:            tail = command[idx + len(marker):].strip()
111:            return tail or None
112:    return None
113:
114:
115:def extract_stop_wrapper_tail(command):
116:    if "spawnSync(process.execPath,[script," not in command:
117:        return None
118:    if "scripts','hooks','run-with-flags.js" not in command:
119:        return None
120:    match = STOP_WRAPPER_RE.search(command)
121:    if not match:
122:        return None
123:    hook_id, script_rel, profiles = match.groups()
124:    parts = ["node", "scripts/hooks/run-with-flags.js", hook_id, script_rel]
125:    if profiles:
126:        parts.append(profiles)
127:    return " ".join(parts)

130:def bootstrap_command(plugin_root, tail):
131:    bootstrap = plugin_root / "scripts" / "hooks" / "plugin-hook-bootstrap.js"
132:    return f'"{NODE_EXE_PATH}" "{win32(bootstrap)}" {tail}'

135:def direct_command(plugin_root, rel_script, *args):
136:    script = plugin_root / rel_script
137:    suffix = " ".join(arg for arg in args if arg)
138:    suffix = f" {suffix}" if suffix else ""
139:    return f'"{NODE_EXE_PATH}" "{win32(script)}"{suffix}'

141:
142:def rewrite_command(command, plugin_root):
143:    tail = extract_inline_tail(command)
144:    if tail:
145:        return bootstrap_command(plugin_root, tail)
146:
147:    tail = extract_stop_wrapper_tail(command)
148:    if tail:
149:        return bootstrap_command(plugin_root, tail)
150:
151:    return command

154:FALLBACK_FUNCTIONS = """function isUsablePluginRoot(candidate) {
155:  const value = typeof candidate === 'string' ? candidate.trim() : '';
156:  return value.length > 0 && fs.existsSync(path.join(path.resolve(value), 'scripts', 'hooks', 'plugin-hook-bootstrap.js'));
157:}
158:
159:function getPluginRoot() {
160:  if (isUsablePluginRoot(process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT)) {
161:    return path.resolve(process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT.trim());
162:  }
163:  if (isUsablePluginRoot(process.env.ECC_PLUGIN_ROOT)) {
164:    return path.resolve(process.env.ECC_PLUGIN_ROOT.trim());
165:  }
166:  return path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
167:}

184:  const raw = readStdinRaw();

194:def patch_bootstrap(plugin_root, verbose=True):
195:    bootstrap = plugin_root / "scripts" / "hooks" / "plugin-hook-bootstrap.js"
196:    if not bootstrap.exists():
197:        return False, f"{bootstrap}: missing bootstrap"
198:
199:    text = bootstrap.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
200:    if "function getPluginRoot()" in text and "const rootDir = getPluginRoot();" in text:
201:        return False, None
202:
203:    if OLD_MAIN_SNIPPET not in text:
204:        return False, f"{bootstrap}: unsupported bootstrap shape"
205:
206:    new_text = text.replace(OLD_MAIN_SNIPPET, FALLBACK_FUNCTIONS + NEW_MAIN_SNIPPET)
207:    backup = bootstrap.with_suffix(bootstrap.suffix + ".pre-wave52-ecc-fix")
208:    if not backup.exists():
209:        backup.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
210:        if verbose:
211:            print(f"BACKUP: {backup}")
212:    bootstrap.write_text(new_text, encoding="utf-8")
213:    return True, None

215:
216:def rewrite(verbose=True):
217:    hooks_files = discover_hooks_json_files()
218:    plugin_roots = discover_plugin_roots()
219:    if not hooks_files:
220:        print("WARN: no ECC hooks.json files found", file=sys.stderr)
221:    rewrites = 0
222:    bootstrap_patches = 0
223:    skips = 0
224:    failures = []
225:
226:    for root in plugin_roots:
227:        changed, failure = patch_bootstrap(root, verbose=verbose)
228:        if failure:
229:            failures.append(failure)
230:        elif changed:
231:            bootstrap_patches += 1
232:
233:    for hooks_json in hooks_files:
234:        try:
235:            data = json.loads(hooks_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
236:        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
237:            failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: json parse error: {exc}")
238:            continue
239:
240:        plugin_root = derive_plugin_root(hooks_json)
241:        changed = False
242:        for command_ref in iter_command_refs(data):
243:            old_command = command_ref["command"]
244:            new_command = rewrite_command(old_command, plugin_root)
245:            if new_command != old_command:
246:                command_ref["command"] = new_command
247:                changed = True
248:
249:        if not changed:
250:            skips += 1
251:            if verbose:
252:                print(f"SKIP (already correct): {hooks_json}")
253:            continue
254:
255:        backup = hooks_json.with_suffix(hooks_json.suffix + ".pre-wave52-ecc-fix")
256:        if not backup.exists():
257:            backup.write_bytes(hooks_json.read_bytes())
258:            if verbose:
259:                print(f"BACKUP: {backup}")
260:        hooks_json.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
261:        rewrites += 1
262:        if verbose:
263:            print(f"REWROTE: {hooks_json}")
264:
265:    if failures:
266:        print("FAIL:", file=sys.stderr)
267:        for failure in failures:
268:            print(f"  - {failure}", file=sys.stderr)
269:        return 1
270:
271:    if verbose:
272:        print(f"DONE: {rewrites} hooks rewritten, {bootstrap_patches} bootstraps patched, {skips} already correct")
273:    return 0
274:
275:
276:def check_bootstrap(plugin_root, failures):
277:    bootstrap = plugin_root / "scripts" / "hooks" / "plugin-hook-bootstrap.js"
278:    if not bootstrap.exists():
279:        failures.append(f"{bootstrap}: missing bootstrap")
280:        return
281:    text = bootstrap.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
282:    if "function getPluginRoot()" not in text or "const rootDir = getPluginRoot();" not in text:
283:        failures.append(f"{bootstrap}: missing fallback getPluginRoot guard")
284:

286:def check_command(command, hooks_json, failures):
287:    if INLINE_BOOTSTRAP_MARKER in command:
288:        failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: inline require(s) bootstrap command remains")
289:    if "node -e" in command:
290:        failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: node -e command remains")
291:    if "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}" in command or "${ECC_PLUGIN_ROOT}" in command:
292:        failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: shell plugin-root token remains")
293:    if "/z/" in command or "\\z\\" in command:
294:        failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: POSIX /z/ or drive-relative \\z\\ remains")
295:
296:    if "plugin-hook-bootstrap.js" in command:
297:        quoted = re.findall(r'"([^"]+)"', command)
298:        if not quoted or win32(NODE_EXE_PATH).lower() != quoted[0].lower():
299:            failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: bootstrap command does not use {NODE_EXE_PATH}")
300:        bootstrap_paths = [q for q in quoted if q.endswith("plugin-hook-bootstrap.js")]
301:        if not bootstrap_paths:
302:            failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: bootstrap command missing quoted bootstrap path")
303:        for bootstrap_path in bootstrap_paths:
304:            if not pathlib.Path(bootstrap_path).exists():
305:                failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: bootstrap missing at {bootstrap_path}")
306:
307:
308:def check():
309:    hooks_files = discover_hooks_json_files()
310:    plugin_roots = discover_plugin_roots()
311:    failures = []
312:
313:    if not pathlib.Path(NODE_EXE_PATH).exists():
314:        failures.append(f"NODE_EXE missing: {NODE_EXE_PATH}")
315:
316:    for root in plugin_roots:
317:        check_bootstrap(root, failures)
318:
319:    for hooks_json in hooks_files:
320:        try:
321:            data = json.loads(hooks_json.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
322:        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
323:            failures.append(f"{hooks_json}: json parse error: {exc}")
324:            continue
325:        for command_ref in iter_command_refs(data):
326:            check_command(command_ref["command"], hooks_json, failures)
327:
328:    if failures:
329:        print("FAIL:", file=sys.stderr)
330:        for failure in failures:
331:            print(f"  - {failure}", file=sys.stderr)
332:        return 1
333:
334:    print(f"OK: {len(hooks_files)} ECC hooks.json files clean; {len(plugin_roots)} plugin roots checked")
335:    return 0

docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:247:Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:249:1. Resolve the actual version (e.g., `npm view @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service version` returns `1.4.7` → install command becomes `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@1.4.7`)
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:250:2. Record both the `@latest` planning intent AND the resolved `@<resolved-version>` in `docs/install-provenance.md` under the install row (per CR-6 D6 risk-acknowledged contract)
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:251:3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:252:4. Re-evaluation trigger: per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"` — the resolved-version pin holds until either a re-install is needed (per upstream changelog or sibling D6 evidence) OR the row's manifest age exceeds N-day re-evaluation cadence
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:254:**Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:256:**Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

1:#!/usr/bin/env node
2:/**
3: * PreToolUse Hook: GateGuard Fact-Forcing Gate
4: *
5: * Forces Claude to investigate before editing files or running commands.
6: * Instead of asking "are you sure?" (which LLMs always answer "yes"),
7: * this hook demands concrete facts: importers, public API, data schemas.
8: *
9: * The act of investigation creates awareness that self-evaluation never did.
10: *
11: * Gates:
12: *   - Edit/Write: list importers, affected API, verify data schemas, quote instruction
13: *   - Bash (destructive): list targets, rollback plan, quote instruction
14: *   - Bash (routine): quote current instruction (once per session)
15: *
16: * Compatible with run-with-flags.js via module.exports.run().
17: * Cross-platform (Windows, macOS, Linux).
18: *
19: * Full package with config support: pip install gateguard-ai
20: * Repo: https://github.com/zunoworks/gateguard
21: */

35:const READ_HEARTBEAT_MS = 60 * 1000;

37:// Maximum checked entries to prevent unbounded growth

39:const MAX_SESSION_KEYS = 50;
40:const ROUTINE_BASH_SESSION_KEY = '__bash_session__';
41:const EDIT_WRITE_HOOK_ID = 'pre:edit-write:gateguard-fact-force';
42:const BASH_HOOK_ID = 'pre:bash:gateguard-fact-force';

45:const DESTRUCTIVE_BASH = /\b(rm\s+-rf|git\s+reset\s+--hard|git\s+checkout\s+--|git\s+clean\s+-f|drop\s+table|delete\s+from|truncate|git\s+push\s+--force(?!-with-lease)|git\s+commit\s+--amend|dd\s+if=)\b/i;

49:function normalizeEnvValue(value) {
50:  return String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
51:}
52:
53:function isGateGuardDisabled() {
54:  if (normalizeEnvValue(process.env.GATEGUARD_DISABLED) === '1') {
55:    return true;
56:  }
57:
58:  return ECC_DISABLE_VALUES.has(normalizeEnvValue(process.env.ECC_GATEGUARD));
59:}

61:function sanitizeSessionKey(value) {
62:  const raw = String(value || '').trim();
63:  if (!raw) {
64:    return '';
65:  }
66:
67:  const sanitized = raw.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
68:  if (sanitized && sanitized.length <= 64) {
69:    return sanitized;
70:  }
71:
72:  return hashSessionKey('sid', raw);
73:}
74:
75:function hashSessionKey(prefix, value) {
76:  return `${prefix}-${crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(value)).digest('hex').slice(0, 24)}`;
77:}
78:
79:function resolveSessionKey(data) {
80:  const directCandidates = [data && data.session_id, data && data.sessionId, data && data.session && data.session.id, process.env.CLAUDE_SESSION_ID, process.env.ECC_SESSION_ID];
81:
82:  for (const candidate of directCandidates) {
83:    const sanitized = sanitizeSessionKey(candidate);
84:    if (sanitized) {
85:      return sanitized;
86:    }
87:  }
88:
89:  const transcriptPath = (data && (data.transcript_path || data.transcriptPath)) || process.env.CLAUDE_TRANSCRIPT_PATH;
90:  if (transcriptPath && String(transcriptPath).trim()) {
91:    return hashSessionKey('tx', path.resolve(String(transcriptPath).trim()));
92:  }
93:
94:  const projectFingerprint = process.env.CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR || process.cwd();
95:  return hashSessionKey('proj', path.resolve(projectFingerprint));
96:}

98:function getStateFile(data) {
99:  if (!activeStateFile) {
100:    const sessionKey = resolveSessionKey(data);
101:    activeStateFile = path.join(STATE_DIR, `state-${sessionKey}.json`);
102:  }
103:  return activeStateFile;
104:}
105:
106:function loadState() {
107:  const stateFile = getStateFile();
108:  try {
109:    if (fs.existsSync(stateFile)) {
110:      const state = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(stateFile, 'utf8'));
111:      const lastActive = state.last_active || 0;
112:      if (Date.now() - lastActive > SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS) {
113:        try {
114:          fs.unlinkSync(stateFile);
115:        } catch (_) {
116:          /* ignore */
117:        }
118:        return { checked: [], last_active: Date.now() };
119:      }
120:      return state;
121:    }
122:  } catch (_) {
123:    /* ignore */
124:  }
125:  return { checked: [], last_active: Date.now() };
126:}
127:
128:function pruneCheckedEntries(checked) {
129:  if (checked.length <= MAX_CHECKED_ENTRIES) {
130:    return checked;
131:  }
132:
133:  const preserved = checked.includes(ROUTINE_BASH_SESSION_KEY) ? [ROUTINE_BASH_SESSION_KEY] : [];
134:  const sessionKeys = checked.filter(k => k.startsWith('__') && k !== ROUTINE_BASH_SESSION_KEY);
135:  const fileKeys = checked.filter(k => !k.startsWith('__'));
136:  const remainingSessionSlots = Math.max(MAX_SESSION_KEYS - preserved.length, 0);
137:  const cappedSession = sessionKeys.slice(-remainingSessionSlots);
138:  const remainingFileSlots = Math.max(MAX_CHECKED_ENTRIES - preserved.length - cappedSession.length, 0);
139:  const cappedFiles = fileKeys.slice(-remainingFileSlots);
140:  return [...preserved, ...cappedSession, ...cappedFiles];
141:}
142:
143:function saveState(state) {
144:  const stateFile = getStateFile();
145:  let tmpFile = null;
146:  try {
147:    fs.mkdirSync(STATE_DIR, { recursive: true });
148:
149:    let mergedChecked = Array.isArray(state.checked) ? state.checked : [];
150:    let mergedLastActive = typeof state.last_active === 'number' ? state.last_active : 0;
151:
152:    try {
153:      if (fs.existsSync(stateFile)) {
154:        const diskState = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(stateFile, 'utf8'));
155:        if (Array.isArray(diskState.checked)) {
156:          mergedChecked = Array.from(new Set([...diskState.checked, ...mergedChecked]));
157:        }
158:        if (typeof diskState.last_active === 'number') {
159:          mergedLastActive = Math.max(mergedLastActive, diskState.last_active);
160:        }
161:      }
162:    } catch (_) {
163:      /* ignore malformed or transient disk state */
164:    }
165:
166:    const finalState = {
167:      checked: pruneCheckedEntries(mergedChecked),
168:      last_active: Math.max(mergedLastActive, Date.now())
169:    };
170:
171:    // Atomic write: temp file + rename prevents partial reads
172:    tmpFile = `${stateFile}.tmp.${process.pid}.${crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`;
173:    fs.writeFileSync(tmpFile, JSON.stringify(finalState, null, 2), 'utf8');
174:    try {
175:      fs.renameSync(tmpFile, stateFile);
176:    } catch (error) {
177:      if (error && (error.code === 'EEXIST' || error.code === 'EPERM')) {
178:        try {
179:          fs.unlinkSync(stateFile);
180:        } catch (_) {
181:          /* ignore */
182:        }
183:        fs.renameSync(tmpFile, stateFile);
184:      } else {
185:        throw error;
186:      }
187:    }
188:    tmpFile = null;
189:    return true;
190:  } catch (_) {
191:    if (tmpFile) {
192:      try {
193:        fs.unlinkSync(tmpFile);
194:      } catch (_) {
195:        /* ignore */
196:      }
197:    }
198:    return false;
199:  }
200:}
201:
202:function markChecked(key) {
203:  const state = loadState();
204:  if (!state.checked.includes(key)) {
205:    state.checked.push(key);
206:    return saveState(state);
207:  }
208:  return true;
209:}
210:
211:function isChecked(key) {
212:  const state = loadState();
213:  const found = state.checked.includes(key);
214:  if (found && Date.now() - (state.last_active || 0) > READ_HEARTBEAT_MS) {
215:    saveState(state);
216:  }
217:  return found;
218:}
219:
220:// Prune stale session files older than 1 hour
221:(function pruneStaleFiles() {
222:  try {
223:    const files = fs.readdirSync(STATE_DIR);
224:    const now = Date.now();
225:    for (const f of files) {
226:      const isStateFile = f.startsWith('state-') && (f.endsWith('.json') || f.includes('.json.tmp.'));
227:      if (!isStateFile) continue;
228:      const fp = path.join(STATE_DIR, f);
229:      try {
230:        const stat = fs.statSync(fp);
231:        if (now - stat.mtimeMs > SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS * 2) {
232:          fs.unlinkSync(fp);
233:        }
234:      } catch (_) {
235:        // Ignore files that disappear between readdir/stat/unlink.
236:      }
237:    }
238:  } catch (_) {
239:    /* ignore */
240:  }
241:})();

243:// --- Sanitize file path against injection ---
244:
245:function sanitizePath(filePath) {
246:  // Strip control chars (including null), bidi overrides, and newlines
247:  let sanitized = '';
248:  for (const char of String(filePath || '')) {
249:    const code = char.codePointAt(0);
250:    const isAsciiControl = code <= 0x1f || code === 0x7f;
251:    const isBidiOverride = (code >= 0x200e && code <= 0x200f) || (code >= 0x202a && code <= 0x202e) || (code >= 0x2066 && code <= 0x2069);
252:    sanitized += isAsciiControl || isBidiOverride ? ' ' : char;
253:  }
254:  return sanitized.trim().slice(0, 500);
255:}
256:
257:function normalizeForMatch(value) {
258:  return String(value || '')
259:    .replace(/\\/g, '/')
260:    .toLowerCase();
261:}
262:
263:function isClaudeSettingsPath(filePath) {
264:  const normalized = normalizeForMatch(filePath);
265:  return /(^|\/)\.claude\/settings(?:\.[^/]+)?\.json$/.test(normalized);
266:}
267:
268:function isReadOnlyGitIntrospection(command) {
269:  const trimmed = String(command || '').trim();
270:  if (!trimmed || /[\r\n;&|><`$()]/.test(trimmed)) {
271:    return false;
272:  }
273:
274:  const tokens = trimmed.split(/\s+/);
275:  if (tokens[0] !== 'git' || tokens.length < 2) {
276:    return false;
277:  }
278:
279:  const subcommand = tokens[1].toLowerCase();
280:  const args = tokens.slice(2);
281:
282:  if (subcommand === 'status') {
283:    return args.every(arg => ['--porcelain', '--short', '--branch'].includes(arg));
284:  }
285:
286:  if (subcommand === 'diff') {
287:    return args.length <= 1 && args.every(arg => ['--name-only', '--name-status'].includes(arg));
288:  }
289:
290:  if (subcommand === 'log') {
291:    return args.every(arg => arg === '--oneline' || /^--max-count=\d+$/.test(arg));
292:  }
293:
294:  if (subcommand === 'show') {
295:    return args.length === 1 && !args[0].startsWith('--') && /^[a-zA-Z0-9._:/-]+$/.test(args[0]);
296:  }
297:
298:  if (subcommand === 'branch') {
299:    return args.length === 1 && args[0] === '--show-current';
300:  }
301:
302:  if (subcommand === 'rev-parse') {
303:    return args.length === 2 && args[0] === '--abbrev-ref' && /^head$/i.test(args[1]);
304:  }
305:
306:  return false;
307:}

311:function editGateMsg(filePath) {
312:  const safe = sanitizePath(filePath);
313:  return [
314:    '[Fact-Forcing Gate]',
315:    '',
316:    `Before editing ${safe}, present these facts:`,
317:    '',
318:    '1. List ALL files that import/require this file (use Grep)',
319:    '2. List the public functions/classes affected by this change',
320:    '3. If this file reads/writes data files, show field names, structure, and date format (use redacted or synthetic values, not raw production data)',
321:    "4. Quote the user's current instruction verbatim",
322:    '',
323:    'Present the facts, then retry the same operation.'
324:  ].join('\n');
325:}

329:  return [
330:    '[Fact-Forcing Gate]',

332:    `Before creating ${safe}, present these facts:`,
333:    '',
334:    '1. Name the file(s) and line(s) that will call this new file',
335:    '2. Confirm no existing file serves the same purpose (use Glob)',
336:    '3. If this file reads/writes data files, show field names, structure, and date format (use redacted or synthetic values, not raw production data)',
337:    "4. Quote the user's current instruction verbatim",
338:    '',
339:    'Present the facts, then retry the same operation.'
340:  ].join('\n');
341:}

344:  return [

348:    '',

352:    '',

362:    '',

364:    '2. What this specific command verifies or produces',
365:    '',
366:    'Present the facts, then retry the same operation.'
367:  ].join('\n');
368:}

370:function withRecoveryHint(message, hookIds = [EDIT_WRITE_HOOK_ID]) {
371:  const disableTargets = hookIds.map(hookId => `\`${hookId}\``).join(' or ');
372:  return [
373:    message,
374:    '',
375:    `Recovery: if GateGuard is blocking setup or repair work, run this session with \`ECC_GATEGUARD=off\` or add ${disableTargets} to \`ECC_DISABLED_HOOKS\`.`
376:  ].join('\n');
377:}

379:// --- Deny helper ---
380:
381:function denyResult(reason, options = {}) {
382:  const includeRecoveryHint = options.includeRecoveryHint !== false;
383:  const hookIds = Array.isArray(options.hookIds) && options.hookIds.length > 0 ? options.hookIds : [EDIT_WRITE_HOOK_ID];
384:  return {
385:    stdout: JSON.stringify({
386:      hookSpecificOutput: {
387:        hookEventName: 'PreToolUse',
388:        permissionDecision: 'deny',
389:        permissionDecisionReason: includeRecoveryHint ? withRecoveryHint(reason, hookIds) : reason
390:      }
391:    }),
392:    exitCode: 0
393:  };
394:}
395:
396:function allowWithStateWarning() {
397:  return {
398:    stderr: '[Fact-Forcing Gate] GateGuard state could not be persisted; allowing this operation to avoid a permanent retry loop. Check GATEGUARD_STATE_DIR or filesystem permissions.',
399:    exitCode: 0
400:  };
401:}

403:// --- Core logic (exported for run-with-flags.js) ---
404:
405:function run(rawInput) {
406:  let data;
407:  try {
408:    data = typeof rawInput === 'string' ? JSON.parse(rawInput) : rawInput;
409:  } catch (_) {
410:    return rawInput; // allow on parse error
411:  }
412:
413:  if (isGateGuardDisabled()) {
414:    return rawInput;
415:  }
416:
417:  activeStateFile = null;
418:  getStateFile(data);
419:
420:  const rawToolName = data.tool_name || '';
421:  const toolInput = data.tool_input || {};
422:  // Normalize: case-insensitive matching via lookup map
423:  const TOOL_MAP = { edit: 'Edit', write: 'Write', multiedit: 'MultiEdit', bash: 'Bash' };
424:  const toolName = TOOL_MAP[rawToolName.toLowerCase()] || rawToolName;
425:
426:  if (toolName === 'Edit' || toolName === 'Write') {
427:    const filePath = toolInput.file_path || '';
428:    if (!filePath || isClaudeSettingsPath(filePath)) {
429:      return rawInput; // allow
430:    }
431:
432:    if (!isChecked(filePath)) {
433:      if (!markChecked(filePath)) {
434:        return allowWithStateWarning();
435:      }
436:      return denyResult(toolName === 'Edit' ? editGateMsg(filePath) : writeGateMsg(filePath));
437:    }
438:
439:    return rawInput; // allow
440:  }
441:
442:  if (toolName === 'MultiEdit') {
443:    const edits = toolInput.edits || [];
444:    for (const edit of edits) {
445:      const filePath = edit.file_path || '';
446:      if (filePath && !isClaudeSettingsPath(filePath) && !isChecked(filePath)) {
447:        if (!markChecked(filePath)) {
448:          return allowWithStateWarning();
449:        }
450:        return denyResult(editGateMsg(filePath));
451:      }
452:    }
453:    return rawInput; // allow
454:  }
455:
456:  if (toolName === 'Bash') {
457:    const command = toolInput.command || '';
458:    if (isReadOnlyGitIntrospection(command)) {
459:      return rawInput;
460:    }
461:
462:    if (DESTRUCTIVE_BASH.test(command)) {
463:      // Gate destructive commands on first attempt; allow retry after facts presented
464:      const key = '__destructive__' + crypto.createHash('sha256').update(command).digest('hex').slice(0, 16);
465:      if (!isChecked(key)) {
466:        if (!markChecked(key)) {
467:          return allowWithStateWarning();
468:        }
469:        return denyResult(destructiveBashMsg(), { includeRecoveryHint: false });
470:      }
471:      return rawInput; // allow retry after facts presented
472:    }
473:
474:    if (!isChecked(ROUTINE_BASH_SESSION_KEY)) {
475:      if (!markChecked(ROUTINE_BASH_SESSION_KEY)) {
476:        return allowWithStateWarning();
477:      }
478:      return denyResult(routineBashMsg(), { hookIds: [BASH_HOOK_ID] });
479:    }
480:
481:    return rawInput; // allow
482:  }
483:
484:  return rawInput; // allow
485:}
486:
487:module.exports = { run };

2:/**
3: * Executes a hook script only when enabled by ECC hook profile flags.

5: * Usage:
6: *   node run-with-flags.js <hookId> <scriptRelativePath> [profilesCsv]
7: */

9:'use strict';
10:
11:const fs = require('fs');
12:const path = require('path');
13:const { spawnSync } = require('child_process');
14:const { isHookEnabled } = require('../lib/hook-flags');

16:const MAX_STDIN = 1024 * 1024;
17:
18:function readStdinRaw() {
19:  return new Promise(resolve => {
20:    let raw = '';
21:    let truncated = false;
22:    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
23:    process.stdin.on('data', chunk => {
24:      if (raw.length < MAX_STDIN) {
25:        const remaining = MAX_STDIN - raw.length;
26:        raw += chunk.substring(0, remaining);
27:        if (chunk.length > remaining) {
28:          truncated = true;
29:        }
30:      } else {
31:        truncated = true;
32:      }
33:    });
34:    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolve({ raw, truncated }));
35:    process.stdin.on('error', () => resolve({ raw, truncated }));
36:  });
37:}
38:
39:function writeStderr(stderr) {
40:  if (typeof stderr !== 'string' || stderr.length === 0) {
41:    return;
42:  }
43:
44:  process.stderr.write(stderr.endsWith('\n') ? stderr : `${stderr}\n`);
45:}
46:
47:function emitHookResult(raw, output) {
48:  if (typeof output === 'string' || Buffer.isBuffer(output)) {
49:    process.stdout.write(String(output));
50:    return 0;
51:  }
52:
53:  if (output && typeof output === 'object') {
54:    writeStderr(output.stderr);
55:
56:    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(output, 'stdout')) {
57:      process.stdout.write(String(output.stdout ?? ''));
58:    } else if (!Number.isInteger(output.exitCode) || output.exitCode === 0) {
59:      process.stdout.write(raw);
60:    }
61:
62:    return Number.isInteger(output.exitCode) ? output.exitCode : 0;
63:  }
64:

69:function writeLegacySpawnOutput(raw, result) {
70:  const stdout = typeof result.stdout === 'string' ? result.stdout : '';
71:  if (stdout) {
72:    process.stdout.write(stdout);
73:    return;
74:  }
75:
76:  if (Number.isInteger(result.status) && result.status === 0) {
77:    process.stdout.write(raw);
78:  }
79:}

81:function getPluginRoot() {
82:  if (process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT && process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT.trim()) {
83:    return process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT;
84:  }
85:  return path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');

88:async function main() {
89:  const [, , hookId, relScriptPath, profilesCsv] = process.argv;
90:  const { raw, truncated } = await readStdinRaw();
91:
92:  if (!hookId || !relScriptPath) {
93:    process.stdout.write(raw);
94:    process.exit(0);
95:  }
96:
97:  if (!isHookEnabled(hookId, { profiles: profilesCsv })) {
98:    process.stdout.write(raw);
99:    process.exit(0);
100:  }
101:
102:  const pluginRoot = getPluginRoot();
103:  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(pluginRoot);
104:  const scriptPath = path.resolve(pluginRoot, relScriptPath);
105:
106:  // Prevent path traversal outside the plugin root
107:  if (!scriptPath.startsWith(resolvedRoot + path.sep)) {
108:    process.stderr.write(`[Hook] Path traversal rejected for ${hookId}: ${scriptPath}\n`);
109:    process.stdout.write(raw);
110:    process.exit(0);
111:  }
112:
113:  if (!fs.existsSync(scriptPath)) {
114:    process.stderr.write(`[Hook] Script not found for ${hookId}: ${scriptPath}\n`);
115:    process.stdout.write(raw);
116:    process.exit(0);
117:  }
118:
119:  // Prefer direct require() when the hook exports a run(rawInput) function.
120:  // This eliminates one Node.js process spawn (~50-100ms savings per hook).
121:  //
122:  // SAFETY: Only require() hooks that export run(). Legacy hooks execute
123:  // side effects at module scope (stdin listeners, process.exit, main() calls)
124:  // which would interfere with the parent process or cause double execution.
125:  let hookModule;
126:  const src = fs.readFileSync(scriptPath, 'utf8');
127:  const hasRunExport = /\bmodule\.exports\b/.test(src) && /\brun\b/.test(src);
128:
129:  if (hasRunExport) {
130:    try {
131:      hookModule = require(scriptPath);
132:    } catch (requireErr) {
133:      process.stderr.write(`[Hook] require() failed for ${hookId}: ${requireErr.message}\n`);
134:      // Fall through to legacy spawnSync path
135:    }
136:  }
137:
138:  if (hookModule && typeof hookModule.run === 'function') {
139:    try {
140:      const output = hookModule.run(raw, {
141:        hookId,
142:        pluginRoot,
143:        scriptPath,
144:        truncated,
145:        maxStdin: MAX_STDIN
146:      });
147:      process.exit(emitHookResult(raw, output));
148:    } catch (runErr) {
149:      process.stderr.write(`[Hook] run() error for ${hookId}: ${runErr.message}\n`);
150:      process.stdout.write(raw);
151:    }
152:    process.exit(0);
153:  }
154:
155:  // Legacy path: spawn a child Node process for hooks without run() export
156:  const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [scriptPath], {
157:    input: raw,
158:    encoding: 'utf8',
159:    env: {
160:      ...process.env,
161:      CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT: pluginRoot,
162:      ECC_PLUGIN_ROOT: pluginRoot,
163:      ECC_HOOK_ID: hookId,
164:      ECC_HOOK_INPUT_TRUNCATED: truncated ? '1' : '0',
165:      ECC_HOOK_INPUT_MAX_BYTES: String(MAX_STDIN)
166:    },
167:    cwd: process.cwd(),
168:    timeout: 30000
169:  });
170:
171:  writeLegacySpawnOutput(raw, result);
172:  if (result.stderr) process.stderr.write(result.stderr);
173:
174:  if (result.error || result.signal || result.status === null) {
175:    const failureDetail = result.error
176:      ? result.error.message
177:      : result.signal
178:        ? `terminated by signal ${result.signal}`
179:        : 'missing exit status';
180:    writeStderr(`[Hook] legacy hook execution failed for ${hookId}: ${failureDetail}`);
181:    process.exit(1);
182:  }
183:
184:  process.exit(Number.isInteger(result.status) ? result.status : 0);
185:}
186:

188:  process.stderr.write(`[Hook] run-with-flags error: ${err.message}\n`);

54:  },
55:  {

67:    run: rawInput => runBuildComplete(rawInput),
68:  },

71:function readStdinRaw() {
72:  return new Promise(resolve => {
73:    let raw = '';
74:    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');
75:    process.stdin.on('data', chunk => {
76:      if (raw.length < MAX_STDIN) {
77:        const remaining = MAX_STDIN - raw.length;
78:        raw += chunk.substring(0, remaining);
79:      }
80:    });
81:    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolve(raw));
82:    process.stdin.on('error', () => resolve(raw));
83:  });
84:}
85:
86:function normalizeHookResult(previousRaw, output) {
87:  if (typeof output === 'string' || Buffer.isBuffer(output)) {
88:    return {
89:      raw: String(output),
90:      stderr: '',
91:      exitCode: 0,
92:    };
93:  }
94:
95:  if (output && typeof output === 'object') {
96:    const nextRaw = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(output, 'stdout')
97:      ? String(output.stdout ?? '')
98:      : !Number.isInteger(output.exitCode) || output.exitCode === 0
99:        ? previousRaw
100:        : '';
101:
102:    return {
103:      raw: nextRaw,
104:      stderr: typeof output.stderr === 'string' ? output.stderr : '',
105:      exitCode: Number.isInteger(output.exitCode) ? output.exitCode : 0,
106:    };
107:  }
108:
109:  return {
110:    raw: previousRaw,
111:    stderr: '',
112:    exitCode: 0,
113:  };
114:}
115:
116:function runHooks(rawInput, hooks) {
117:  let currentRaw = rawInput;
118:  let stderr = '';
119:
120:  for (const hook of hooks) {
121:    if (!isHookEnabled(hook.id, { profiles: hook.profiles })) {
122:      continue;
123:    }
124:
125:    try {
126:      const result = normalizeHookResult(currentRaw, hook.run(currentRaw));
127:      currentRaw = result.raw;
128:      if (result.stderr) {
129:        stderr += result.stderr.endsWith('\n') ? result.stderr : `${result.stderr}\n`;
130:      }
131:      if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
132:        return { output: currentRaw, stderr, exitCode: result.exitCode };
133:      }
134:    } catch (error) {
135:      stderr += `[Hook] ${hook.id} failed: ${error.message}\n`;
136:    }
137:  }
138:
139:  return { output: currentRaw, stderr, exitCode: 0 };
140:}
141:
142:function runPreBash(rawInput) {
143:  return runHooks(rawInput, PRE_BASH_HOOKS);
144:}
145:
146:function runPostBash(rawInput) {
147:  return runHooks(rawInput, POST_BASH_HOOKS);
148:}
149:
150:async function main() {
151:  const mode = process.argv[2];
152:  const raw = await readStdinRaw();
153:
154:  const result = mode === 'post'
155:    ? runPostBash(raw)
156:    : runPreBash(raw);
157:
158:  if (result.stderr) {
159:    process.stderr.write(result.stderr);
160:  }
161:  process.stdout.write(result.output);
162:  process.exit(result.exitCode);
163:}
164:
165:if (require.main === module) {
166:  main().catch(error => {
167:    process.stderr.write(`[Hook] bash-hook-dispatcher failed: ${error.message}\n`);
168:    process.exit(0);
169:  });
170:}

[31;1mInvalidOperation: [31;1mCannot set property. Property setting is supported only on core types in this language mode.[0m

codex
**Verdict: NEEDS-REVISION**

The foundation is close, but I would not approve it as “stable/no silent fallback” yet. The main blockers are telemetry semantics, weak hard-gate assertions, and avoidable per-tool hook overhead.

**Findings / Prescribed Edits**

1. **Postcommit/prepush “completed=0” is expected by design, but bad telemetry. Conf 0.93.**  
`codex_postcommit_review.py` appends `main_entered` at `:817` and a detached `launched` row at `:879-884`; it never waits and has no code path that writes `completed`. `codex_prepush_review.py` mirrors this at `:792` and `:843-847`. Current local evidence is not “170 launches”: I see `codex_postcommit_reviews.jsonl` = 98 rows, 97 `main_entered`, 1 `launched`, 0 `completed`; `codex_prepush_reviews.jsonl` = 80 rows, all `main_entered`. The actual verdict for the one launched run lives in `codex_review_HEAD_18fdbf0f.txt` and its `.stream.jsonl`.  
**Edit:** add a small detached supervisor or SessionStart collector that reads the stream/outfile and appends `status: "completed" | "failed"` to `codex_postcommit_reviews.jsonl` / `codex_prepush_reviews.jsonl`. Also rename `main_entered` to `dispatch_entered` or add filter-skip rows so dashboards stop treating dispatch markers as launches. Anthropic documents async hooks as non-blocking and unable to control behavior after launch, so this must be telemetry-side, not a normal async hook return. 

2. **`eee.ps1` HARD-GATE still has silent skip paths. Conf 0.90.**  
At `tools/eee.ps1:330` and `:351`, both rewriters run only if script and Python exist; missing rewriter scripts silently skip. At `scripts/codex-plugin-hooks-rewrite.py:96-98` and `:130-132`, “no hooks.json found” returns 0. ECC has the same false-pass shape at `scripts/ecc-plugin-hooks-rewrite.py:219-220` and `:308-335`. `T0.2` checks plugin names only, not boolean `true` (`eee.ps1:204-208`). `T0.5` checks `codex` exists, not version, `CODEX_HOME`, or the `deep-review-exec` profile (`eee.ps1:235-239`). `T0.6` only requires 3 MCPs while `.mcp.json` now declares 5 (`eee.ps1:241-266`).  
**Edits:** fail hard when either rewriter script is missing; make both `--check` modes return nonzero on zero discovered hook files when the plugin is enabled; verify enabled plugin values are `true`; add a no-network config assertion that `Z:/claude-sota-installed-state/.codex/config.toml` contains `[profiles.deep-review-exec]`; validate all 5 MCP server names plus `npx`/`uvx` command presence. Claude project MCP config and env expansion are officially supported, so the gate should validate the real declared project config. 

3. **Codex profile is structurally correct, but not smoke-proven. Conf 0.82.**  
`config.toml:56-63` defines `model = "gpt-5.5"`, `model_reasoning_effort = "xhigh"`, read-only sandbox, never approval, fast tier. Codex docs support `-p/--profile` for `codex exec`, and the config reference supports profile-scoped model, reasoning effort, sandbox, approval, and service tier. GPT-5.5 supports `xhigh` reasoning.   
**Caveat:** T1 auto-spawn overrides the profile sandbox with `--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox` at `codex_t1_consult_gate.py:1045`; postcommit/prepush do not.  
**Edit:** add a cheap launcher check for `CODEX_HOME/config.toml` and optionally a manual smoke command after launch, not on every edit.

4. **CWC `bash Z:/...` form is acceptable, but Python lookup can fail open. Conf 0.78.**  
The hook command form in `settings.json:80,85,96,107` is reasonable on Windows/Git Bash, and Claude hook command fields support shell execution with bash as default.   
**Issue:** `track-read.sh:6` and `verify-gate.sh:8` call `python`; if Git Bash cannot resolve `python`, parsing fails and both scripts effectively no-op.  
**Edit:** set `PYTHON_BIN=Z:/venvs/claude/Scripts/python.exe` in settings env and use `"${PYTHON_BIN:-python}"` in those scripts, or hardcode the venv Python.

5. **ECC hook set should be pruned for this runtime. Conf 0.86.**  
Essential/keep: `pre:config-protection`, `pre:mcp-health-check` and `post:mcp-health-check` but narrow matcher to `mcp__.*`, `post:edit:accumulate` + `stop:format-typecheck` if JS/TS edits are expected, `SessionStart`, and possibly `pre:bash:dispatcher` only for `block-no-verify`.  
Overhead/disable in Z-drive CC: `pre/post:observe`, `pre/post:governance-capture` while `ECC_GOVERNANCE_CAPTURE=0`, `suggest-compact`, `doc-file-warning`, `design-quality-check`, `console-warn`, `session-activity-tracker`, `stop:evaluate-session`, `stop:cost-tracker`, `desktop-notify`, GateGuard fact-force.  
**Edit:** expand `ECC_DISABLED_HOOKS` in `settings.json:10` or switch `ECC_HOOK_PROFILE` toward `minimal` plus re-enable only targeted gates.

6. **C: tools are not inherently against Anthropic guidance. Conf 0.84.**  
Anthropic’s install guidance is about Claude Code binary integrity/signing and update behavior, not requiring `codex`, `git`, `node`, `npm`, `python`, `gh`, or `uvx` to live on the same drive.  The cross-drive bugs are real plugin/Windows path-resolution defects: #285 is POSIX `/c/...` plugin root handed to native `node.exe` from a different drive, and #287 is Node `spawn("codex")` not resolving Windows `.cmd` shims.  Your installed `app-server.mjs` already has the HEAD-style `shell: process.platform === "win32"` and `windowsHide` fix locally, so C: tools are acceptable if PATH is stable.

7. **Strict T1 on every edit is too expensive; keep warn-T1 by default. Conf 0.88.**  
Current settings do not set `CODEX_T1_GATE_STRICT`, so T1 is warn-only: it scans consult-pair files and returns context, not a 5-30s Codex call. If strict is enabled, `codex_t1_consult_gate.py:1864-1884` auto-spawns Codex and denies first attempt until the sidecar verdict lands.  
**Edit:** keep WARN globally; make STRICT opt-in only for high-risk files: `.claude/settings.json`, `.mcp.json`, `tools/eee.ps1`, Codex config, hook scripts, and rewriters. Use T2/precommit and postcommit for broad coverage instead.
